### 10.10 Shared public target-card selection and outcome for Steal / Burning Bridges — newly approved multiplayer UX correction (2026-10-15)

**User-observed defect:** When a player uses **Steal (顺手牵羊)** or **Burning Bridges / Dismantle (过河拆桥)**, the operating player can see their private target-card chooser and the card taking/discarding effect, but **other participants cannot understand what is occurring**. Fix the **shared public journey on every authenticated viewer screen**, without showing private Hand cards or private live picker choices. This task is **new public-progress/outcome UX**, **not** a request to reimplement the already delivered centre/park ROOT Stratagem (§10.2.B) or change these cards' game rules. It overrides any earlier §10.2 source-review **NO CHANGE** classification as to the newly reported observer visual gap only.

**Source-grounded baseline from current `ux-v2` inspection:** `app/api/rooms/route.ts` already publicly projects `pendingTargetCard` containing the action's `kind`, `sourceId`, `actorId` and `targetId` after Negation settles; `CurrentAction.targetCardSelection` is the **actor-only** authoritative eligible-key picker. The actor's `app/page.tsx` `TargetCardPicker` correctly shows anonymous `hand:n` positions and genuinely public Equipment/Judgement candidates, but no equivalent small observer progress surface is presently driven from `pendingTargetCard`. The existing server emits proof-backed `publicDismantleSettlement` on a genuine discard CardFace, and `publicStealSettlement` on a generic settlement **message** that intentionally omits the taken card's identity. The validated `game/presentation-v2.ts` and `game/presentation-snapshot.ts` projections therefore establish ROOT/settlement identity but do **not currently express the complete public origin-zone/movement narrative**. `app/page.tsx` has settlement candidates for both and supplies their original root `cardFace` to `PublicCardDisplay`; the new work should **extend** this rather than mount an unrelated duplicate presenter. This is a targeted source-inspection finding, **not** a claim that the full remote UI was reproduced on real phones in this review.

#### 10.10.1 Non-negotiable everyone-can-follow sequence

| Stage | Actor-only interaction | Public state visible to every other player |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **1. Committed root** | Original physical Steal/Burning Bridges card is accepted and uses existing §8.7 centre→true actor Seat/Dock Policy-B journey | Everyone sees same genuine root card, real source and targeted player. Preserve applicable **§9 private all-seat Negation** before the effect; no target card is available for selection during an unresolved/cancelled Negation window. |
| **2. Authoritative choosing** | Only authorized actor sees the existing clickable black/gold Hand/Equipment/Judgement `TargetCardPicker`, with anonymous target Hand positions | All other viewers see a **small, nonblocking, publicly truthful indicator** near the target Seat or just above the existing root journey: `A is choosing one card from B…` plus the real card-effect name. Show real source and target with a gentle progress affordance. Do **not** copy the interactive picker, reveal which zone/card/position is under the pointer, identify Hand cards, or publish selection/revision/hover/tap events. The status persists across polls and reconnect while the server's target-card pending state is active. |
| **3A. Committed Steal settlement** | Server removes **exactly one** real card from target Hand/Equipment/Judgement and adds it to the actor's **private Hand** | All viewers see the source→target connection through text and a **single target-origin→actor-destination visual move** or compact zone-attached transition. If taken from hidden Hand, move an **anonymous face-down back** only; no art/name/suit/rank/cardId/hand position or private future Hand contents. Show proven public Hand counts updating. If taken from a **public Equipment or Judgement zone**, the exact previously public CardFace may be shown **only when a genuine typed public removal/transfer proof identifies it**; remove it from target's displayed zone and briefly show its movement to actor's Hand without leaving a permanent face-up "played" card or exposing later private Hand state. Text: `A stole one card from B's Hand/Equipment/Judgement` only if that zone is server-proven; otherwise `A stole one card from B`. |
| **3B. Committed Burning Bridges settlement** | Server removes **exactly one** genuine card from target Hand/Equipment/Judgement and places it in actual Discard | All viewers see the target-zone→**non-visible Discard anchor** transition and a short authentic `A discarded one card from B's [proven zone]` result. The current server's public `discard` CardFace event can support the original real physical art **after authoritative commitment** when game rules/public projection permit revealing that discarded card; no identity is exposed **while selecting**. Public Equipment/Judgement selections use their already-public identity. If the card's identity is not authorized for a specific case, use a back/quantity notice instead, not a guessed face. Never resurrect the permanently visible Deck/Discard piles forbidden by §8.2. |
| **4. Settlement/retirement** | Correct source and target zones/Hand state updated | Every viewer sees one coherent completion cue with exact card owner/result; no merely cryptic `STEAL RESOLVED` / `BURNING BRIDGE RESOLVED` without explaining who gained/discarded from whom. Original root parks/retains true author's public history as §8/§10.7 requires, while the taken/discarded card never falsely parks as a played Stratagem at the wrong Seat. Retire transient progress/motion after settlement; preserve accessible chronological public history. |

**Important before/after privacy boundary:** It is public that **A is acting on B**; it is **not public** which hidden card, hidden Hand index, currently hovered target, or even which eligible zone A is considering before confirmation. Even if a chosen source zone becomes publicly provable after server commit, **never extrapolate an opponent's remaining Hand identities**. A face-up Equipment/Judgement card was already public; moving it into a Hand does not grant perpetual inspection into that Hand. Do not expose `CurrentAction`, private target-selection keys, secret Negation provider/Pass state, or session tokens to other viewers.

**Two different physical outcomes, not one generic movement:** Steal physically TRANSFERS a card to the actor's Hand; Dismantle physically DISCARDS it. Destination, count changes, animation lifetime and log wording must correspond exactly. A stored real CardFace can never simultaneously appear as an Equipment miniature, a moving public card and a permanently "played" card; transient animation ownership must not duplicate the live server card zone. Do not transform the *root Steal/Burning Bridges card* into the selected/removed target card or create a second imaginary root.

#### 10.10.2 One shared observer UX, no rule changes or double presentation

- **Source of truth:** Public choosing state must derive from the authenticated server's `pendingTargetCard` (or the equivalent proven public semantic projection), not from `room.isMyAction`, local modal open, a stale cached `CurrentAction`, or guesses from `room.actionPlayerId`. All clients (actor, victim and unaffected seats) use the same committed root/action/settlement IDs; only the genuine actor receives editable picker selections.
- **Proof extension for animated zone transitions:** Inspect and minimally extend the authoritative Steal/Dismantle settlement payload to carry a **publicly safe** `sourceZone` (`hand|equipment|judgement`) and **actual affected-card owner/source/target**. For *public* Equipment/Judgement movement, include the exact genuine public CardFace/card ID with necessary causal/root/event linkage; for **hidden-Hand Steal**, NEVER expose actual card identity, `hand:n` index or private IDs in public JSON, timeline, snapshot, DOM, attributes, accessible text, debug or presentation telemetry. A typed public opaque **movement-event ID** may drive the one anonymous back. Do not rely on client diffing zone arrays/Hand counts or guessing the movement's cause. When proof is unavailable, show the factual quantity/result without an invented physical animation.
- **Discard identity policy:** The current Dismantle server emits one public `discard` CardFace; before reusing this for target-Hand discards, confirm its public identity semantics against the project rules. The new UX must never increase disclosure relative to authoritative allowed public events. If the rules differ by origin zone, encode that server-side and test all viewers' **raw wire JSON**, not just screen text.
- **Existing presenter first:** Reuse the root public card journey, short H3-readable public sentence, Seat/Dock/hidden-discard animation anchors and `publicDismantleSettlement`/`publicStealSettlement` projections. The public status is a **compact seat-related phase label**, not a giant card-selection dialog, a relationship graph/arrow, a permanent overlaid deck/discard or a second simultaneous large central CardFace. Ensure the affected-card motion does not violate §8.7: **only a newer committed public response/counter CardFace** interrupts an existing Policy-B centre reveal, and a target-card private selection or mere settlement is NOT such a response. New physical disposition may animate locally without stealing root centre or blocking controls.
- **Obsolete/cancelled:** Cancelled/Negated Steal or Burning Bridges shows its real Negation outcome and **no** choosing/transfer/discard animation; invalid/stale/double submissions never produce ghost effects. If source/target dies, Equipment is replaced, selected Hand index moves, a new independent root starts or a prior public interaction retires, follow actual server settlement/cleanup; do not show a former pending status indefinitely. Preserve causal fix R2.
- **Performance:** Public phase cue must be readable for non-actor viewers who did not open the picker; don't allow a one-poll flash to hide the only transfer result. Use server event IDs for replay/dedup and reasonable bounded read time, without artificially extending the current turn or locking a new legal response. Never emit user-specific interactive selection to a spectator.

### 10.11 Stargazing whole-card touch drag — NEW user-reported iPhone usability fix, 2026-10-15

**User's observed problem:** Zhuge Liang's **Stargazing (观星)** currently feels as if cards can be rearranged only by tapping `Move / Top / Bottom / Earlier / Later` buttons. **Required visible interaction:** directly **touch-and-hold anywhere on the genuine CardFace, then drag the physical card** to either deck zone or a new position in its current zone; menu buttons remain as a secondary accessible alternative. This is a usability/regression task for the **existing PRIVATE deck-reorder dialog (§5.2)**, not a new Hero rules feature and **NOT** a §10.9 public skill-cost Frame. Do not put the private Stargazing faces in public centre/seat animation.

**Source inspection — existing implementation is PARTIAL, do not reimplement blindly:** `app/page.tsx` `PrivateDeckReorderDialog` already has `DeckDrag`, pointer capture, a 260ms touch hold, insertion markers, top/unassigned/bottom arrangements, edge auto-scroll, keyboard/screen-reader Move menu and the authoritative `private_deck_reorder` submit. However `beginDrag()` explicitly **returns for touch unless the original touched element is inside `[data-deck-touch-handle]`**. The touch handle is `.deck-reorder-drag-grip` in `app/sequence-overrides.css`, only **27×27 CSS px** at the card corner with `touch-action: none`; the rest of `.deck-reorder-card-face` uses `touch-action: pan-x pan-y` and is not a touch drag origin. Thus the code currently **does not support ordinary whole-card long-press drag** even though §5.2 had described generalized touch-hold dragging. This identifies a **specific source-supported UX gap**, not independent real-device reproduction; perform a live device/browser test before claiming repaired.

#### 10.11.1 Exact gesture contract

1. **Whole-card gesture, 1/2/N cards:** On the authorized actor's private Stargazing dialog, a finger placed on **any non-control point within the displayed actual physical CardFace**, including the art, title or lower edge, is a valid touch source. A short deliberate long-press (approximately 200–300ms; calibrate to reliable iOS Safari behavior) **visibly picks up that card** with a lifted/dragging treatment. The user can then move it freely without releasing; releasing over a valid zone commits the local rearrangement. Pointer/mouse on desktop may press-and-drag directly without a long hold. The tiny optional grip must **not** be a prerequisite; it may remain as a visual affordance but should not consume a large portion of a 64–94px card.
2. **Three genuine interactive zones, not just buttons:** From any of **REVEALED CARDS (unassigned)**, **TOP OF DECK**, **BOTTOM OF DECK**, the dragged card can be dropped into either other zone or repositioned **before, between or after** cards in its current zone. Empty top/bottom rows accept drops across a comfortable whole-row hit area. Highlight the actual target zone and show a stable, visible insertion caret/gap before release; the lifted card follows the finger without obstructing the drop hit test. The three rows remain in the same vertical order as §5.2.
3. **Ordering is semantically important:** TOP left→right = first actual future draw to later draws; BOTTOM left→right = the existing server-required bottom order after the remaining deck. Verify the actual `topCardIds` and `bottomCardIds` arrays after moves: inserting ahead of another card must preserve the exact visual order, not reverse it or shift by an off-by-one error when dragging within one row. Drop into another row removes the original position. Each original card ID exists in exactly one of `unassigned / top / bottom` throughout every action.
4. **Cancellation and scroll behavior:** Native page/contained-row scrolling and intended vertical dialog scroll should not accidentally begin a drag on a quick tap/swipe. Once an explicit hold has activated drag, prevent competing native pan/context menu/text selection so iOS does not cancel or steal the pointer; allow the user to reach off-screen positions by edge auto-scroll. Before activation, a quick swipe to scroll should behave normally where practical; scroll gestures should **not** move a card. A `pointercancel`, Escape, drag outside valid zones, panel disappearance, server action-revision change, lost capture, multi-finger interference or stale submission leaves the exact previous arrangement and does not leak a card to another zone. Account for CSS `touch-action` and pointer capture on the **whole CardFace**, not only grip, without breaking vertical scrolling or the Move menu.
5. **Honest feedback and accessibility:** Show a readable short instruction `Hold and drag a card to Top or Bottom; drag again to change order`. Active card visibly lifts; target zone / insertion point shows contrast and non-color cue, with a succinct live confirmation of its resulting zone and 1-based position. Preserve real card art, rank and suit in this *private* dialog only. Keep the `Move` menu and Earlier/Later/Top/Bottom/Return accessible for keyboard, screen-reader and users unable to drag; `COMPLETE STARGAZING` remains reachable without dragging. Focus return/announcement and `prefers-reduced-motion` are respected.
6. **Server, privacy and completion unchanged:** No mutation or requests on drag; submit only once the user clicks **COMPLETE STARGAZING** using existing `{providerId:"private_deck_reorder",topCardIds,bottomCardIds}` contract and server-projected `minTop/maxTop`. Completion disabled while any card remains unassigned, constraints fail or an action is busy/stale. All-to-Top / all-to-Bottom are valid only if the authoritative constraint allows. **All true card identities remain private to Zhuge Liang's viewer**: other clients, spectator/debug presentation, timeline, DOM/ARIA outside the owner dialog and server public events never learn the four drawn/private revealed cards or local drag positions.
7. **Responsive minimum:** At 390×844 and short 390×700 iPhone viewport, a card remains easily touch-holdable and moving between vertically separated zones never clips off-screen or drops onto action buttons. Also test 360/440/480px phone and different number of revealed cards (including five when server affords). Validation is mobile-only per the product owner's instruction; do not require desktop visual/pointer checks. Preserve §5.2 four-card recognizability, horizontal contained scrolling when necessary, safe-area insets, sticky/accessible submit, full control reachability and no overscroll of main game Hand/Seats.

### 10.12 UI-4-F — Diao Chan Lust: public activity chip, local guidance, single cost Frame and generated-Duel history (user iPhone feedback, 2026-10-15)

**Review verdict:** §10.6 UI-4 original technical delivery was green but **NOT USER ACCEPTED for Lust**. Five real iPhone screenshots show: (1) Local Dock Guidance wastes room on \`YOUR DECISION\`, \`Test Player 2's turn\` and \`LOCAL CANCEL / Change your selection\`, plus a Cancel button; important \`Lust order\` is squeezed BELOW the main guidance box. (2) The true paid **5♣ Hex Mark** appears alone at battlefield centre while \`LUST\` and player-order prose float at the TOP RIGHT, sometimes over the top Hero row. (3) The skill/cost disappears instead of parking under Diao Chan. (4) First real Duel Attack gets stuck centrally rather than parking under its actual author. (5) A later Attack replaces the earlier Attack and Lust history altogether. Gameplay may work but **the unified UX2 Frame + proof-backed, actor-owned history does not**. **Beauty Outshining the Moon is NOT reviewed yet**; no UI-4 blanket acceptance.

**Design supersession:** The user rejected a full-width public turn/status bar under the top-row Heroes because it conflicts with their parked played cards. User-approved alternative is ONE **short, visually quiet translucent capsule at the LOWER BATTLEFIELD edge**, immediately ABOVE and OUTSIDE the Local Dock. This is distinct from **H4-A/B/C public event narration** centred on the battlefield: the capsule means *who publicly acts / what is pending*; H4 tells *what was committed*; LOCAL Guidance tells *what I personally must do*. A skill Frame is physically separate from the standing capsule. Do not reuse H4 copy/banner as turn-owner chip or move H4 back to upper-right.

#### 10.12.1 One persistent PUBLIC ACTIVITY CHIP — with privacy-safe ownership

**Precise content rules:**
- **Normal Play Phase:** \`Test Player 2's turn\`, derived from authoritative PUBLIC \`room.turnSeat\`/actual turn state, identical across independent viewers. Update on each true turn transition; don't repeat in Local Guidance.
- **Public Duel exchange / other PUBLIC single-actor response:** \`Waiting for Test Player 3 · Duel\` or \`Waiting for Test Player 4 · Attack\` ONLY if current actor and public action are proven by the shared server projection. Do not confuse original turn owner, activating Diao Chan and the next Duel responder.
- **Secret UX2 simultaneous Negation Plan A:** ALL clients see \`Waiting for Negation responses\`. Never publish any responder's name, submitted/pass state, response count, priority, card ownership or inferred Negation availability; not even when one player has already submitted. Existing 8s/all-explicit server settlement remains untouched.
- **No public actor proof / settling:** \`Resolving…\` or hide the capsule rather than infer from PRIVATE \`currentAction.reason\`, viewer-specific \`pendingNegation.submitted\` or client guesses. On game finished, show \`Match complete\` (or hide), not a stale player turn.
- **Full-time within game:** status persists and updates from authoritative room state during new turns and response phases for all viewers; it is not a 2.4-second toast. When no real change happens, no flicker.

**Projection/code:** Build a pure \`derivePublicActivityStatus\` on strictly PUBLIC room fields/\`PresentationV2\` or extend \`app/api/rooms/route.ts\` with an allowlisted \`publicActivity\` if current per-viewer room shape cannot guarantee identical data. Never drive a shared public label from \`buildDecisionPresentation\`'s viewer-private isWaiting/reason. Test identical public text on independent player tokens. Fail closed, and retain \`aria-live="polite"\` without announcing every poll.

**Geometry:** One-line content-sized badge, ~11–12px readable typography, ~22–30px height, subtle black/translucent background and thin gold border, \`pointer-events:none\`; NOT a full-width panel. Anchor at **bottom centre of \`.play-table\`**, above Local Guidance and left of bottom-right countdown as needed. Dynamically reserve actual occupied region for any local/side Seat-attached Duel/Negation history, public centre Frame, H4 announcement, equipment, controls and timer; if lower middle occupied use the nearest stable alternative **in the SAME lower public corridor**, not a top-of-table jump or overlay. Prefer shortening truthful copy to 1 line, avoid shrink-to-unreadable font or stretching. This chip must not follow a moving physical CardFace. Test 390×844/390×700/440×894/480×900 and 4/6/8/10 Seat topology. If space genuinely impossible, temporarily hide instead of cover essential controls, without losing authoritative status. Inspect actual bounding rectangles on mobile.

#### 10.12.2 Trim the LOCAL prompt and make active LUST tap cancel an UNCOMMITTED local selection

**Source verified (2026-10-15, \`ux-v2\`):**
- \`app/page.tsx\` \`.console-guidance .decision-status\` renders \`YOUR DECISION\` in a \`<small>\` and also renders \`LOCAL CANCEL\` + text; actual \`Lust order\` is a separate sibling \`<small role="status">\` after \`.console-guidance-row\`, explaining why it falls outside the main panel. \`[data-action-slot="cancel"]\` separately renders Cancel for active Hero selection.
- \`heroSkillButtons\` handles skill activation and \`resetLocalTargetFlow("active-skill")\`; its \`selection.type === "target"\` branch currently returns early when already active, so never remove an existing Cancel surface before proving that repeated tap of LUST can clear the still-uncommitted state.

**Required:** Remove **only decorative** \`YOUR DECISION\` top row and redundant \`LOCAL CANCEL / Change your selection before submitting\` in this Lust local selection. Move repeated public \`Test Player N's turn\` / publicly known waiting actor OUT of the private Guidance into the public chip. Keep only the local actionable instruction (e.g. \`Select 1 card and 2 Duel participants\`) plus \`Lust order: [first actual selected target] plays Attack first; [second] responds.\` **INSIDE \`.decision-status\`**, before optional selection count. Source target order from currently selected first/second IDs, never from viewer-suggested names or stale state; show no invented order with fewer than two selected. Once server confirms, public first actor comes from \`publicDiaoChanNotice.firstActorId\`.

During *uncommitted* Lust selection, clicking **the already-active LUST skill button again** MUST clear just local \`kingSkillId\`, \`activeSkillSelectionState\`, selected card and selected targets (reuse \`resetLocalTargetFlow("active-skill")\` safely), no server action/card consumption. After verifying this, hide **the redundant Lust-specific Cancel button** in \`[data-action-slot="cancel"]\`; do not remove unrelated necessary Cancel/Clear for Borrowed Sword, target card picker, mandatory trigger, or responses. Confirm still required to submit; don't allow cancel to undo a committed ability. Maintain 44px touch and accessible \`aria-pressed\`, valid response controls/TAKE DAMAGE/Pass/Confirm/System Menu/keyboard. Avoid removing actual \`RESPONSE SUBMITTED\` state feedback if it carries useful meaning.

**Human user acceptance (2026-10-17): L2 USER-ACCEPTED/CLOSED.** In the dedicated L2 iPhone review, the user said **“这项设计检查通过了”**. The 440px screenshot shows a legible attack order inside the main Guidance, while the LUST button/cancel behavior is covered by the user's explicit acceptance. Two independent findings from that screenshot/trace are tracked as H2-G1-F (large circular opponent Inspect icons) and L5-F (fast public Duel result/trigger notice), **neither of which reopens L2**.

#### 10.12.3 ONE centred genuine LUST COST Frame, not separate toast + isolated card

**Approved unified Frame style:** Reuse accepted Da Qiao §10.5.3 Deflection framed physical payment and §10.9.1 A COST. On a *committed* Lust, display ONE centred visual unit containing:
- \`LUST\` / \`Diao Chan · LUST\` as a small header;
- a concise verified description: the two real Duel participants and **which actual player must play Attack FIRST**;
- the **ONE exact publicly committed Hand or Equipment cost CardFace**, e.g. paid 5♣ Hex Mark in the screenshot, not a fake printed physical Duel card. Skill avatar/portrait stays in the existing real player Seat, not duplicated in centre.

**Actual source separation confirmed:** Server \`app/api/rooms/route.ts\` \`execution.outcome.kind==="lust"\` calls \`addCardGroupEventWithPublicEffect\` with \`effectId:"diao_chan_lust"\`, \`effectClass:"COST"\`, \`activationOwnerId\` and publicly proven \`materials\`, then emits a **separate** \`publicDiaoChanNotice\` \`ACTIVATION\` message with \`sourceId\`, two participants and \`firstActorId\`. \`game/presentation-v2.ts\` has both COST classification and independently proven \`diaoChanSkillNoticesFor\`. \`app/page.tsx\` \`diaoChanNoticeCopy\` dispatches a separate \`effectNotice\`, and \`app/globals.css\` \`.effect-notice\` has \`top:8px; right:...\`, narrow width and its own 2400ms lifetime. The material card takes a different event presentation path.

**Requirement:** Correlate these **exact** proofs by typed authoritative cost-event ID ↔ activation ID (or a shared unique skill activation identity), actor/source, effect, owned physical card ID, movement and target order. If no explicit cross-event relationship already exists, add a minimal allowlisted server public proof field; DO NOT join by nearest timestamp/log string, card image coincidence, or viewer Hand. Verify exact Hand/Equipment owner and actual \`DISCARD\` move; visual parking is history only and does not mutate physical zones. The Frame's whole group follows §8.7 Policy-B big centre hold up to 4s (new genuine Attack can interrupt immediately), ~380ms centre→Seat park. Once the Frame truly owns the ACTIVATION notice, suppress **only** its duplicate right-floating \`effect-notice\` by event ID; keep unrelated notices and separate later verified Lust settlement. If cost/notice proof fails, show a SINGLE truthful short public notice without creating a false-cost CardFace. No public cost material before confirmation and no private Hand/Negation leak. Reuse **accepted H3 text style** and existing H4-A/B/C stable independent narration band rather than binding text to Frame animation.

#### 10.12.4 Skill-generated Duel is NOT a played Duel card: add new proof variant and retain ALL author-attached history

**Proven blocker:** \`game/presentation-v2.ts duelExchangeFor()\` requires \`continuation.damageCards\` to contain exactly ONE genuine **printed Duel** card and an actual public \`type:"card", action:"play", kind:"Duel"\` root. Server \`app/api/rooms/route.ts duelAttackResponseProof()\` ALSO demands that printed-Duel root and returns no proof without it. However \`beginLustDuel()\` creates a \`continuation.lust\` with **activation message \`rootEventId\`**, owner, two players, firstActorId, and no printed Duel card. Therefore generic normal-Duel \`DuelCardJourney\` cannot currently establish the genuine skill Duel responses/history; do not attempt CSS-only fix or fabricate a Duel CardFace.

**Solution:** Add a strict typed \`LUST_GENERATED_DUEL\` / \`PresentationLustDuelExchange\` root variant with **real skill activation message event ID + paid COST event/CardFace** and proven \`interactionId\`, \`rootFrameId\`, skill owner, two participants, firstActorId and actual response IDs/ordinals. Emit/validate a separate, explicitly labelled Lust-response proof (e.g. \`relation:"LUST_DUEL_EXCHANGE"\`) for server-committed Attack response(s) with true physical actor/owner, including valid played-as Attack/delegation if such behavior is server-supported. Keep \`PresentationDuelExchange\`'s normal printed-card validation entirely unchanged. Fail closed on mismatched/ambiguous cost or response proof—NO fabricated physical Duel or Attack from pending intent or alternating player assumptions; no modification to damage/HP/server action deadlines.

**Exact lifecycle, ALL viewers:**
1. **#1** genuine LUST Frame (skill name, short order and 1 paid actual cost card) reveals in centre, then its **real paid card/compact Frame thumbnail parks under Diao Chan's real Hero Seat**, or in her local played-card Dock when she is viewer. Paid card actually stays in authoritative Discard; parking is *visual provenance*, not ownership mutation.
2. **#2** first REAL committed Attack in Lust duel reveals big alone at centre and then parks under the **actual author/physical payer Seat/Dock**, preserving #1 in Diao Chan's author history.
3. **#3, #4…** each subsequent REAL committed Attack interrupts the prior centre reveal if necessary, then parks under that actual author, APPENDED rather than replacing earlier same-author cards. Cross-player unique visible ordinals #1, #2… explain order. Prior cost and Attacks remain legible for the **entire active Lust Duel**, including when response windows continue. **Exactly one large physical card/group in centre at a time**, no central horizontal collection and no invented printed Duel root.
4. When actual server settlement proves who failed to play Attack and whether damage was applied/prevented, show ONE concise truthful final result; keep the complete cost+Attack thumbnails readable for about **2 seconds after settlement** (or until a genuine new independent interaction supersedes), then retire coherently. No indefinitely stuck tiny Attack in centre, no older cards disappearing while same Duel unresolved.
5. Preserve §8.1 actual actor Seat topology: top-row history stacked vertically under original author's Hero/equipment row; side seats fan inward; local authored history flows horizontally above local Dock. Overflow must be scrollable/inspectable inside real safe bounds, not cover protected HP/Hero/Hand/H4 text/public chip/timers. View switch, late join, refresh and polling reconstruct already committed history parked **without replaying old 4s holds or duplicating physical cards**. A client should only reveal new verified event(s) big once. Real physical IDs, public privacy and first-actor proof remain authoritative; no fake Pass or damage from pending choice.

**Important UI-4 boundary:** The earlier User-ACCEPTED printed Duel §8.1 history stays working; don't relax its physical root. This new Lust root extends it for a DIFFERENT typed source; reuse only safe motion, actor anchor and chronology code. A single valid attack committed while generated Duel active should not be rejected merely because \`Duel\` was not physically played. Confirm \`duelAttackResponseProof\` truly carries a distinguishable author vs effect/source ID, not just the player whose turn is current. Screenshots 3–5 must be regression cases for absent skill cost and lost first response.

#### 10.12.4.1 L4-F — Duel played-card history must NOT disappear when opponent Inspector opens (IMPLEMENTED / DEPLOYED — PENDING USER PHONE RE-REVIEW 2026-10-23)

**User evidence / important root distinction:** iPhone images IMG_3270 / IMG_3271 and local trace `wtk-game-ux-trace-2026-10-17T13-37-08.728Z.json` show an active **ordinary printed Duel** from Cao Cao against Zhang Liao, NOT the separate skill-generated LUST root. The actual room roster has Sima Yi, Xiahou Dun, Cao Cao and Zhang Liao; no Diao Chan; the projected displayed action carries `rootCardKind:"Duel"`, `motionType:"duel"`, and a genuine printed Duel root `#1` followed by authentic Attack responses `#2–#5`. These files demonstrate a **shared Duel presenter/Inspector regression**; they do not confirm or refute the separate LUST skill-cost branch. That original phone review was not accepted at the time; the current code correction below is technically delivered and remains pending a fresh phone re-review.

**Directly corroborated disappearance (NOT merely an old-version hypothesis):** Screenshot IMG_3270 has the opponent Zhang Liao INSPECT window open but no centre/Seat/Dock Duel history although Duel response mode remains live. Trace has a corresponding prolonged region around 2026-10-17 13:36:20–13:37:01 UTC of `serverInteraction.stage:"DUEL_EXCHANGE"` and real response IDs still in `recentPublicAttackDodgeEvents` / `validatedTimelineEvents`, while `publicCardDisplay.enabled:false`, `ready:false`, `temporarilyBlockedBy.expandedOpponent:true`, `publicCardDisplayMounted:false` and `cardInstances:[]`. After selecting another viewer/closing Inspector, the same root and real Attack history reappear (IMG_3271; trace 13:37:01.639 then 13:37:02.151 UTC). This is evidence of a **rendering/mount/authorization gate tied to an inspection UI state**, not deletion of committed server Attack events. Trace is a finite rolling extract: 321 kept entries and `droppedEntryCount:3222`, so other intermittent routes remain to be checked.

**Confirmed root cause on the tested pre-fix Worker:** `app/page.tsx` combined `opponentInspectionPresentation || expandedOpponentId` with transient target-preview/card-picker/group blockers in `rootActionTemporarilyBlocked`. That Boolean fed `publicCardDisplayEnabled`, so informational opponent `INSPECT` disabled the public physical journey even though committed public timeline events remained unchanged. The fix keeps only already parked Duel/LUST history mounted in an inert passive presentation during opponent Inspect. It does not surface a new centre card or announcement while Inspect is open, does not intercept Inspector touches, and lets the ordinary journey resume with the same local state when Inspect closes. Existing target/card/group blockers remain independent.

**Normative acceptance and fix boundary:**
1. Opening/closing any public opponent Inspector (e.g. Zhang Liao), own Hero/Hand CardDetails or a legitimate inspect-only preview MUST **not remove, reset, replay, or relocate** already committed real Duel/LUST cost and Attack history. INSPECT can layer in front and intercept its own touch area but does not own/remove the public physical journey. If the user opens a full modal during a live response, keep publicly safe previously PARKED cards mounted and legible where not covered by the actual panel, and return the same true timeline after closing without a fresh four-second reveal. Do not place blocked buttons behind an active modal.
2. Use committed `room.timeline`, true `rootEventId`, authentic `cardId`, `actualAuthorId`, `interactionId` and proven public response sequence to preserve history. Keep a single centre large card at most, honest chronological ordinals and real owner Seat/Dock, current card first-reveal Policy-B lifecycle, 380ms movement and ~2s settled read window. A viewer SWITCH, polling revision/checkpoint or opening/closing Inspect must not cause a history reset. Never fabricate Duel/Attack or leak uncommitted response/private Hand.
3. Verify collision and accessibility: opponent Inspector content should remain readable on top of history; dismiss/same-opponent re-tap closes per separate R4-I approved design, without treating Inspect as a target selection or submitting Attack; legal current-response controls remain usable when inspection is closed. Do not undo previously user-accepted §8.5 Inspector nested detail navigation.
4. **Shared-root regression guard:** Repeat with a **real printed Duel** (the observed failing path) AND a separate **Diao Chan LUST generated Duel** with one authentic skill COST + at least three real alternating Attack cards. Inspector open midway, close, change viewer, reload and continue to settlement; no disappearing history before authoritative settlement retirement, no stale ghosts afterward. Check normal Attack/Dodge, genuine Negation, Da Qiao and Group/AOE history where renderer gates overlap; do not re-open accepted H4 geometry or alter game rules.

Implementation `74b344d5b298907744918c6eb96ec5f8375e1484` keeps parked public history mounted and noninteractive while an opponent Inspector is open; it preserves true event/card/actor IDs and does not change rules or viewer-private projections.

#### 10.13.1 P0-A — Multi-select Hand must have one source of truth and simultaneous visible selection

1. In app/page.tsx Hand renderHandCard, derive selected IDs from the **active interaction mode**: discardSelected for discard, activeSkillSelectedCardIds for active skill cost, serpentSelected for multi-card conversion/response, selected for genuine single select. Maintain existing selection precedence and authoritative eligibility. Do not mix a retained single-card selected/details state with discard selection.

Selected count, confirm enablement and visible class must use the exact same ID set. Changing to another card must **not** clear or visually lower the first selected card. Tapping the same card again deselects only that card; exceeding required max adds nothing.
3. In effective app/sequence-overrides.css, expose every selected slot (overflow visible, non-clipping lift and paint order). Apply a consistent **visible** lift approx 14–22 CSS px on small phones plus warm border/glow/checkmark, not just an inner −5px transform clipped by the rail. Give selected slots raised z-index without allowing the newest card to hide a previously selected neighbor. Keep all selected card faces discernible when overlapped, with touch/pan hit regions and DETAILS unaffected. Distinguish persistent selected state from hover, focus and details-selected state; do not let “only one details preview” collapse multi-selection feedback.
4. On phase/actionRevision/viewer switch or authoritative hand mutation, reconcile/remove obsolete selected IDs with existing clearing rules. On submit keep card IDs stable; disable double-submit while busy; on server rejection recover visibly without falsely claiming cards discarded. No client-generated card state, discard-limit logic, or new HTTP action.
5. **Hard acceptance:** On a real discard phase needing two cards, after selecting card A, A remains raised; after selecting B, **A and B remain raised simultaneously** and 2/2 appears; deselect A leaves only B raised and 1/2; select C shows B+C and server receives exactly [B,C] only on explicit Confirm. #### 10.13.2 P0-B — Promote authoritative public waiting/turn/resolution into the battlefield status

**User outcome:** At a glance, all seats see “whose turn”, “who is truly blocking progress”, and “what public activity”; the local dock answers only “what can I do now?” Make the existing PublicActivityChip prominent in the lower battlefield, outside the dock, rather than adding a second permanent banner. Target approximately 14–17px bold phone main text, 11–13px subordinate, min 44px visual/touch-height budget where appropriate, ornate thin gold border with muted black/green background. The status text need not be tappable.

**Authority and privacy decision matrix** (derive on server-proven public fields only; no inference from DOM/turn owner/timeline/names or viewer-private hand/response flags):

| Authoritative condition | Public battlefield plaque | Local Guidance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Normal active turn, no other decision | “Test Player 2's turn” (optional public phase) | If local actor: legal play/discard/confirm instruction; otherwise no duplicated global turn notice |
| Proven ordinary one-seat blocking Duel exchange | **“Waiting for Test Player 3”** + **“Duel Exchange”** (or concise “Duel”) on ALL observers, including current participant | For Test Player 3: own “Play Attack / Decline” control instructions; other viewers: unobtrusive “No action needed” or collapse non-action instruction |
| Proven one-seat Attack/Dodge, Judgement, rescue, mandatory skill choice | “Waiting for [public actor]” + proven public response/stage name; if local actor, “Your response” may be a local-only visual accent but public wording stays consistent | Only local legal response and selection info |
| Engine automatically continuing, no proven blocking actor | **“Resolving [public effect]…”**, not the name of a guessed waiter | No false local wait action; hide disabled instructions as appropriate |
| Multi-seat private/shared Negation window | **“Waiting for Negation responses”** identically on all seats; never disclose who has responded, whose hand contains Negation or who is next | Only viewer-private legal options/own submission state |
| Settled or finished | Brief proven outcome if required; then next turn or “Match complete” | Reset stale local instruction; no stale action owner |

Current game/public-activity.ts deliberately accepts PresentationSnapshot rather than CurrentAction or local flags; **preserve this public-only contract**. Broaden covered proven CHOICE/SPECIAL states (including genuine Duel after a skill-generated root) *only when* interaction semantics, stable decision actor and effect/stage align. When missing/mismatched, fail closed to generic “Resolving…” rather than inventing a player. Do not leak private Negation seats. The current Duel branch requires phase=response and snapshot.duelExchange matching actor; test whether screenshot's otherwise-correct local decision state has a different stable checkpoint/phase. Diagnose projection-to-chip mismatch before loosening the proof.

Move globally relevant status copy OUT of the local console-guidance (.decision-status waiting small/strong branch) and INTO this plaque; do not move local card/target requirements, self-only skills, local selection count, or decline buttons to the public table. Preserve one polite, non-spamming live announcement and avoid two competing aria-live regions repeating identical actor messages. Reposition/enlarge app/public-activity-chip.tsx placeChip and effective .public-activity-chip CSS with measured safe bounds: must not obscure lower-right hourglass, public announcement/H4, Duel history, Seats, Inspector, stage menu or the Local Dock. Prefer a central safe lower band with honest collision fallback (compact but **not** microscopic), preserve pause/resize/reduced-motion/reprojection. Do not fake stability with a timer.


**Latest source-audit clarification, 2026-10-15:** `game/public-activity.ts` uses `snapshot.duelExchange` for the named ordinary Duel response, while newly proved generated Lust exchange is modeled independently as `PresentationV2.lustDuelExchange`; this is a **specific hypothesis** for public chip falling back to `Resolving…`. Require multi-token traces verifying public stage, actor and timing before implementing a new state projection. Private local `CurrentAction.reason` must NOT be copied to public simply because it contains the desired words.

#### 10.13.2.1 U13-B-F — privacy-first unconditional public status during Yue Jin Dauntless turn-end (USER-SELECTED 2026-10-18; IMPLEMENTED / DEPLOYED — PENDING USER REVIEW)

**User finding and decision:** The four-viewer reproduction showed that a public timeline message `Alice may use Dauntless` and a public status transition from the next player's turn to `Resolving…` occur only when Yue Jin holds a Basic card. The public `PresentationSnapshot` does not prove a safe decision actor. On 2026-10-18 the user selected **privacy-first: use an unconditional public status**. This supersedes the earlier two-level named-wait proposal below; it does not revoke the accepted §10.13.2 U13-B chip placement.

**Normative public contract:**
1. Whenever a living Yue Jin is off-turn at a turn-end boundary, the server opens the same private response window whether she holds zero or one or more eligible Basic cards. With an eligible Basic card, only Yue Jin receives the existing Dauntless option and cost candidates. Without one, only Yue Jin receives Pass. The other viewers receive no actor ID, actor name, Dauntless eligibility, private response deadline/countdown, or Dauntless-specific timeline message.
2. During either private window every viewer sees the same neutral `Resolving…` public activity status. Do not derive or publish this from another viewer's `CurrentAction`, hand, provider option, local timer, Hero identity, or DOM. Remove conditional public `may use Dauntless` text. Publish Dauntless only after its authoritative cost/effect is committed.
3. Reuse the existing human response timeout (60 seconds); the actor can Pass immediately. With no eligible Basic card, this can add up to 60 seconds to each non-Yue-Jin turn while a living Yue Jin remains in the game if she does not respond. This is the explicit latency cost of the user's privacy-first choice. Do not change card legality, costs, outcomes, or the accepted U13-B/H4 presentation shell.
4. On Pass, timeout, or activation, clear the private window and continue turn-end processing exactly once; do not retain a stale public wait or emit a false activation.

**Required proof:** use separately authenticated four-player Worker/D1 views for zero and one eligible Basic card; assert the same public status and timeline, no actor/skill/cost/deadline in other viewers' projections, and owner-only Pass/Dauntless actions. Cover activation, Pass, timeout, continuation, refresh/reconnect, and viewer Switch. Keep `derivePublicActivityStatus` public-projection-only; add a public non-actor turn-end status only if supported by the server-owned unconditional window. Preserve the accepted chip shell and require real-phone review after deployment.

### 10.14 UI-4B-F — Diao Chan Beauty Outshining the Moon: public DRAW-1 result and stage-centered notice (2026-10-15 real iPhone)

**Human real-phone decision: UI-4B BEAUTY NOT ACCEPTED; focused corrective work is APPROVED and READY for Coding Agent.** Screenshot IMG_3186.jpeg at 23:55 from Diao Chan's own iPhone: the battlefield shows a top-right floating `BEAUTY / Diao Chan activated Beauty Outshining the Moon.` notification over the upper-player/right battlefield space, while a genuine PRIVATE DRAW panel displays an actual Q♥ Peach to its owner. The bottom independent public activity chip correctly identifies a current turn, but the public skill notification never clearly describes the key result **“drew 1 card” during the visible draw**. User requires proper **stage-central public information placement** and **one truthful explicit public draw outcome**. Do not imply other viewers were shown Q♥; the screenshot comes from the rightful viewer. This is a real UX defect separate from the original Lust correction and the already accepted H3 typography; keep Beauty human acceptance OPEN.

#### 10.14.1 Source proof already exists; current CLIENT sequencing and CSS explain likely screenshot

**Actual code read at latest ux-v2:**
- **Server is already authoritative.** `app/api/rooms/route.ts` Beauty branch after accepted optional `apply_trigger`: invokes `drawCards(...,1,...)`, updates only Diao Chan's actual private Hand, emits one public `publicDiaoChanNotice` `ACTIVATION` with `sourceId` and, **iff exactly one card was actually drawn**, another public `DRAWN` with `rootEventId` pointing to activation, the same `sourceId`, `drawCount:1`, `finalResult:true`; adds separate PRIVATE physical draw event for the actual drawn CardFace. Do not replace/forge the public proof using private card ID, optimistic counter or animation.
- **Projection validates causality.** `game/presentation-v2.ts` `diaoChanSkillNoticesFor()` strictly checks that a Beauty DRAWN notice points to one actual matching Beauty ACTIVATION of the same Diao Chan, with genuine `finalResult`, `drawCount===1`; the public field contains the count but **not the drawn card face**.
- **Correct public English wording ALREADY EXISTS.** `app/page.tsx diaoChanNoticeCopy()` formats `ACTIVATION` as `BEAUTY / Diao Chan activated Beauty Outshining the Moon.` and `DRAWN` as `BEAUTY RESULT / Diao Chan used Beauty and drew 1 card.`
- **Displayed result can lag behind activation.** In `app/page.tsx`, `queuedNotices` iterates public message events and appends each separate proven Beauty event to `effectNotices`. The visible `effectNotice` is the **queue's head**, with a `UI_TIMING.effectNotice` expiry before the following notice is shown. Even when server commits ACTIVATE and DRAWN into the same updated timeline, the iPhone can still see activation-only while the result remains queued. Verify with event ID/head/queue traces; do not assume the second event was missing or delayed on the server. The screenshot is consistent with head-of-queue presentation lag, not evidence that the draw failed.
- **Right-floating CSS is DEFINITE.** The current `.game-shell .play-table > .interaction-safe-zone > .effect-notice` in `app/globals.css` is `top:8px;right:clamp(...)`, `width:min(320px,...)`, causing the exact wrong-side caption. Only Lust COST/Duel notice IDs currently receive special suppression via `publicCardDisplayAction`; Beauty still goes through the independent top-right generic notice. Preserve other proven effect notices when redirecting this skill.

**UI semantics / expected public copy:**
- **Confirmed exactly-one draw (server `DRAWN` proof):** one visible notice `BEAUTY` with main factual copy **`Diao Chan used Beauty and drew 1 card.`** (optionally `Diao Chan · Beauty — drew 1 card` as compact wording). Actor name must come from verified public `sourceId`; do not hard-code Diao Chan's player label if a named player should be shown, and NEVER display `Peach`, `Q♥`, physical ID, suit, rank, Hand index or an opponent's private new card.
- **Committed activation but no linked DRAWN yet:** a short provisional **`Diao Chan activated Beauty`** is allowed only while proof is genuinely pending. Do **not** show `drew 1 card` until linked `DRAWN` exists. If ACTIVATE+DRAWN are present together, **prefer the complete final DRAWN statement immediately**; do not force users to wait through a 2.4s activation toast. If only ACTIVATE appears and DRAWN is later proven, REPLACE/UPGRADE the original text IN PLACE rather than queueing an unrelated second toast.
- **Declined, stale, unsuccessful (zero-card) draw, or unrelated Hero event:** no invented draw result. A declined optional Beauty action should not emit a false activation/success notice; keep other Hero notifications intact.
- **No duplicate noisy labels:** one coherent public skill-event announcement, NOT `BEAUTY` + `BEAUTY RESULT` consecutively with the same two messages. Use a small skill title only as context; present the genuine draw as the main sentence. For screen readers, announce the final proof exactly once and avoid a stale provisional announcement reappearing after polling.

#### 10.14.2 One stage-centered public-event narration layer (coordinate with current H4) and independent PRIVATE DRAW

**Approved placement:** Beauty's PUBLIC status is a stage-wide PUBLIC EVENT, not a physical card, because the skill has **NO publicly committed cost CardFace**. Reuse **H4's canonical centred public-event narration geometry and visual ornament** as a common positioning owner for both genuine card events and proven no-physical-card Hero skill events. Do NOT create a phantom central Beauty CardFace, a top-right `.effect-notice`, a second banner, a standalone huge skill window, or a public picture of drawn Hand. Stage-center alignment should be **≤12 CSS px** from the canonical reading-band center and stable top within **≤16px for unchanged geometry**, consistent with H4's measured safe corridor.

**Crucial private-draw concurrency:** Unlike Attack→Dodge, the Beauty notice can coexist with a real `.private-draw-stage` and its `PRIVATE DRAW / ONLY YOU CAN SEE THESE CARDS` label+card. Reserve the actual PRIVATE DRAW visual rectangle as a protected collider **on that same local viewer**; the public notice sits in the NEAREST stable central battlefield reading band **above the private draw**, with a clear **8–12px gap**, below top Hero/Equipment/Seat/parked physical public history. If the regular H4 card-above position is blocked by the enlarged private draw on a 390×700 viewport, allow a narrowly tested alternative centre sub-band, NOT right docking and NOT overlaying the top Hero. On non-owner views with NO private draw, the public caption uses the same canonical central band.
