#### 10.15.5 Binding implementation contract — physical lifecycle, semantic checkpoint and viewer geometry are separate clocks

**Single-owner rule:** For one validated (roomCode, interactionId, rootFrameId, rootEventId, physicalCardId, actualAuthorId), only one **physical root presentation coordinator** may render the public root. It must persist across NEGATION, GROUP_RESOLUTION participant 1/2/3, damage, optional triggered skill and settlement, even when presentationRevision, checkpointId, participant, response timer, selection branch or notice changes. Build this linkage from PROVEN server/public relationships; never join by latest timeline item, card kind, player name or turnSeat. If evidence of the linkage is missing, fail closed rather than guess. Other unrelated card families may still use their generic fallback; a root already owned/parked by a Journey must **never** return through static fallback.

| Independent clock | Stable authority | Events allowed to change it | Forbidden side effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Physical event identity** | Proven root event + physical card + true author + typed interaction/frame proof | A different genuinely committed physical card | Duplicate or impersonated root |
| **Visual lifecycle** | Same physical event state: UNSEEN → REVEAL → MOVE → PARKED → RETIRED | First public commitment, normal ≈4s reveal, ≈380ms move, real interrupt or retirement | PARKED→REVEAL from a new checkpoint, response, poll or switching viewers |
| **Semantic checkpoint** | PROVEN interaction/decision/current participant/settlement | Server advances group target, Negation, damage, outcome | Restart visual root or move already parked root |
| **Viewer layout epoch** | Viewer ID + measured Seat/Dock/topology + visualViewport | TEST SWITCH, reconnect, resize/rotation, topology changes | Old-view-to-new-view flying animation; wrong author |
| **Public narration** | Latest proven public stage/event copy via H4 | Genuine server-proven semantic stage change | Text follows shrinking root, stale previous-stage notice, false private reveal |

**Q2 / state ownership:** extend one typed Group-root Journey throughout normal Group response and GROUP_RESOLVED, including phases where Negation is not active. Preserve existing exact source/root/card/frame proof, root actor identity, historical genuine Negations and distinct physical response cards. A genuinely NEW committed response card may reveal at centre and interrupt the root; the old root remains PARKED. The old root must not be reselected as a new central event by an unrelated target response or settlement result. Implement arbitration once at a shared presenter boundary, not separately per group stage. Do not merely hide CSS static cards while still mounting duplicate physical owners.

**Q3 / first-paint view barrier:** visual phase/commit history belongs to the root, while layout coordinates belong to the CURRENT viewer. On TEST SWITCH or legitimate layout-epoch change, reproject already PARKED roots to the new measured true-author anchor BEFORE first paint, with transition disabled. Do not animate from cached actualCardRect belonging to another viewer, do not flash at stale coordinates and do not replay 4s because an event is new to the observer. If new anchor is unmeasurable, postpone that card's painting rather than use 0,0 or guess. A newly confirmed physical card in the same, unchanged view still gets the accepted 4s/380ms motion. Test on Attack/Dodge, Duel/Lust and other Journey types too, while keeping Q3 a separate bounded task.

**Q4 / H4 independent public caption:** use one stage-fixed, safely located PublicCardAnnouncement band, sourced only from up-to-date proven public checkpoints. It may change copy as response windows genuinely advance; it must not change its position because a card shrinks, parks or a timer ticks. Retire old-stage copy as soon as authoritative semantic scope changes (e.g. no leftover "Negation can be played" during Group response); do not reveal private submissions. Measure the actual newly deployed H4 implementation, accept existing H3 font/copy, and preserve USER-ACCEPTED U13-B activity chip. H4 centre deviation ≤12px and vertical movement ≤16px apply under unchanged safe geometry; avoid top Seats, current central/parked physical cards, timer, Local Dock and chip.

**Q1 instrumentation additions:** privacy-reviewed diagnostic trace should log paired PROVEN server and presenter IDs and this public-only record per render checkpoint: build SHA, session ID, monotonic elapsed time, room/interaction/frame/checkpoint/revision, root event/card/author, current participant, current viewer/layout epoch, rendererKind/motionType/visualPhase, reveal/move start and elapsed, number + DOM path/rect of every SAME-root full/parked instance, last viewer rect versus current anchor rect, H4 notice event/semantic stage/text key + rect, safe stage/obstacle rects. Redact all private Hand identities, other players' Negation availability/intents, credentials, secret roles. Instrument ahead of behavior changes and avoid treating presentationRevision as a new physical-card identity.

**Deterministic acceptance invariants across each sampled paint:**

1. Exactly one real, newly committed root reveals centrally, moves to proven author once, then remains PARKED during all later target responses; zero old-root central full-size instances after PARKED. New separately committed physical response is allowed centrally.
2. Root presenter retains constant physical ID/author under all server checkpoints and up to final proven retirement; renderer arbitration prevents both a live physical Journey and an independent same-card static fallback.
3. Post-switch PARKED card's **first painted** x/y is its newly measured actual-author anchor (≤2px rounding tolerance), not any interpolated point from previous-view x/y; no re-reveal/clock reset.
4. Exactly one current public narration for a given stage, with current proven copy, H4-safe geometry and no collision with accepted U13-B chip, HP, countdown, Seat histories or Local Hand.
5. Server rules, CurrentAction legal controls, full physical conservation, Policy-B timing, multi-seat Negation Plan A privacy, R1/R2/Da Qiao/Lust and existing accepted UX remain unchanged.

**Mandatory proof:** run a real authenticated 4-client Worker/D1 Barbarian Invasion and Raining Arrows through Negation, 3 sequential participant responses, damage/trigger and final settlement; match the EXACT root/card/author IDs across clients (arrival times need not match exactly). Separately stress TEST SWITCH 1→2→3→4→1, both during initial reveal and after parking; reconnect/late join, slow polls, viewport 390×700, 390×844, captured 440×766, 440×894 and 480×900, both 4-seat top row and 6/8/10 side-seat topology, reduced motion. Capture DOM states/frame rects/caption history rather than rely on smoke or still screenshots alone. Do not confuse a single TEST SWITCH browser with four real clients. Exact-SHA CI/Worker version and tests must be actually observed; if Q1's current explicit ban on local automated tests remains, honour it and label unverified claims. Technical delivery is PENDING HUMAN REVIEW.

**Sequence and task ownership:** Current HANDOVER.md Q1 is still IN PROGRESS, with diagnostic changes reportedly local-only. **Do not reset, overwrite or begin Q2 in place of that uncommitted work.** Q1 → Q2 → Q3 → Q4 are four individually bounded Coding Agent tasks; finish one and then replace the handoff with the next. The first thing Agent must do on resumption is re-read this latest design revision and preserve its local Q1 instrumentation. No implementation, post-fix game verification, new H4 failure or user visual acceptance is claimed by this document revision.


#### 10.15.6 Executable three-surface presentation specification — MUST / MUST NOT (2026-10-17 clarification)

**P0 user requirement in one sentence:** For the **same proven real card**, the (A) central full-sized card, (B) returned small author-attached card and (C) shared public explanatory text must describe one coherent public event across time and viewpoints; they must not independently restart, move between owners, disagree, or resurrect old stages. This section is **normative over cosmetic placement preferences** and can be handed directly to the Coding Agent. The problem is **presentation arbitration and rehydration, not changes to the WTK rules or CurrentAction**.

**Three surfaces have distinct jobs and must NEVER compete for authority:**

| Surface | When it exists | Where it belongs | Exactly what it may display | What it must NEVER do |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **A. Centre REVEAL / MOVE CardFace** | Only for a **newly committed, server-proven physical card** whose event has not already completed its current viewer's reveal/move lifecycle; newer committed cards may interrupt | Central battlefield safe reveal area, then animate toward true author | ONE current full-size real card with real card ID, owner and event ID | Rerender an old parked root because the group advances to the next target; invent a second root from settlement; run again after TEST SWITCH/poll/refresh |
| **B. PARKED miniature/history** | The same physical event after MOVE, or immediately on viewing already-committed history after switching/late joining/reconnecting | Actual **author's** Seat or own Dock in this observer's current topology (global chronological ordering retained) | The same real physical card, shrunk once, with genuine ordinal when applicable | Fly from a previous observer's seat; change owner; grow back into a centre card; disappear merely because next participant must answer |
| **C. Public event narration (H4)** | While there is a **current, proven public stage/event** requiring a shared explanation; update or retire as proven semantic stage changes | Its **own fixed central safe reading band** (not a child of the card's position/transform) | Current proven actor, action, target, response or outcome text; accepted H3 typography/copy | Follow A or B; lag with a prior Negation/target message; duplicate a separate generic toast; overlap A/B, player HP, response countdown, Dock or accepted U13-B status plaque |

**Strict state-transition contract for one root:**

~~~text
PROVEN NEW ROOT [event/card/author] -> REVEAL(max 4000ms by Policy B)
                                  -> MOVE(about 380ms, to THIS viewer's true-author anchor)
                                  -> PARKED (same real card; stay parked through all target decisions)
                                  -> RETIRED (only when proven history-display policy retires it)

PARKED + target-2/3 response = PARKED (no large re-display)
PARKED + GROUP_RESOLVED      = PARKED (no new static large card)
PARKED + presentationRevision/checkpoint changes = PARKED
PARKED + TEST SWITCH / resize = PARKED at recalculated destination, SNAP WITHOUT TRANSITION
PARKED + poll / reconnect / remount = PARKED, never replay by default
NEW different committed response card = its OWN new centre lifecycle; prior root stays PARKED

C: Proven public semantic stage N -> stage N+1 = replace/retire stage-N text;
   C position remains in its safe band and is not coupled to A/B transforms.
~~~

**Timing and interruption:** The intended REVEAL hold is **up to approximately 4 seconds**, and MOVE about **380ms**, not an unconditional 4-second blocking delay. A newer, *genuinely committed and publicly proven* response may interrupt the prior centre reveal; no purely local response intent or transient UI checkpoint may do so. No new card? Then no fresh REVEAL. A network poll, target index advance, damage/skill settlement, stage revision or visual viewport change is **not** a new physical commitment. Reduced-motion users see equivalent identity/placement without forced flight. Do not infer timing/ownership from a DOM timer.

**Cross-player contract using the recorded genuine Barbarian root:**

| State / viewer | Expected A centre | Expected B miniature | Expected C narration |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Viewer 0 first sees root 76af7aee… newly committed | One initial REVEAL, then MOVE | Same root parks under actual source 72383b7b… | Initial **server-proven** public root/action message; H4 safe band |
| Viewer 0 watches target 2, then target 3 respond | **No second root** at centre (new genuine response CardFaces only if proved) | Root d7478792… remains under that same author | Text advances to the newly proven response/target; previous text removed |
| TEST SWITCH viewer 0→1→2→3 during group response | **No root REVEAL restart** | Same physical root **snaps directly** to each new viewer's measured author Seat/Dock, zero flight from prior layout | Current public semantic message reprojects into the viewer's **own safe H4 band**, does not trail the card |
| Group settles | **No 88×128px old-root static fallback** | Root history remains on true author until normal proven retirement | Only current group settlement outcome; obsolete response/Negation message gone |
| Newly connected independent client after root was already public | **No historical replay as fresh commitment** | Reconstructed parked root in the actual author slot according to the existing history policy | Latest proven public stage, not delayed or cached private copy |

**Renderer exclusivity is a functional requirement, not a styling tweak:** At any observation of a given physical root, the system must have a **single effective visual owner** (one typed Journey rather than Journey competing with static-fallback). No full-size static root CardFace is permitted once that root is PARKED, even if a Group settlement produces a different action object; no CSS-only hiding of a duplicate which is still independently driving stale notice state. Display identity must use proven interaction/root/card/author linkage and **must not depend on** checkpointId, presentationRevision, current target seat, current player name, current viewerId or DOM coordinates. Those fields can update **semantics** or **layout**, not reset the root's visual clock.

**Presentation and geometry must be separated:** The root's logical phase/event history survives updates. On a viewerId/Seat topology/visualViewport change, invalidate old coordinates, measure new DOM anchors, and suppress transitions **before painting** any already parked historical card. A client may have one viewer-relative position per layout epoch, but may NEVER interpolate from previous-view x=338px to new-view x=196px for an already PARKED card. If current true-author anchor is unavailable, wait/withhold rendering; never guess location/owner. Same rule applies to legacy Attack/Dodge, Negation, Duel/Lust and other real physical card Journeys, without expanding Q2 beyond Group roots.

**Public narration freshness:** The H4 text's semantic event ID/checkpoint must track current **PROVEN** server interaction state. At confirmed advance, replace the prior stage's visible copy atomically: e.g. “Waiting for Negation” cannot linger when response ownership has advanced to a proven group target; “target 1 responded” cannot still be the visible current instruction while target 2 is acting. The narration's placement is determined by a safe stage band; it must not be recalculated from A's shrinking card or B's parked anchor. When geometry changes (e.g. viewer switch) the safe band may legitimately reproject, but under **unchanged layout** its centre drifts no more than 12px horizontally and 16px vertically. Preserve the separate USER-ACCEPTED U13-B bottom activity chip; H4 provides *event meaning*, U13-B provides *whose action is awaited*.

**Source evidence vs current release:** The uploaded old-build aa71e75 trace proves root REVEAL t=72.729, MOVE t=76.791, PARKED t=77.162, then later same-root full card sightings at t≈87.79 and 116.63; TEST SWITCH records x=338.3→196→53.7→14 for the same parked root. This evidence **does not by itself certify present H4 malfunction** or real four-client simultaneity. Audit the **deployed new SHA**. From latest source, untyped normal Group/settlement candidates and PublicCardDisplay static-fallback remain the main Q1/Q2 suspects; do not claim a bug fixed by specification alone.

**Concrete Coding Agent acceptance gate, not optional:**

1. **Q1 (current only):** Diagnose and log ONE root's stable `rootEventId`, `physicalCardId`, `actualAuthorId`, `interactionId`, `rootFrameId` from PROVEN server projection to chosen renderer, with `rendererKind`, `motionType`, `visualPhase`, `viewer/layoutEpoch` and all same-physical-card DOM paths/rects. Record whether a static fallback actually reappears after PARKED on **latest Worker**. Keep current uncommitted diagnostics safe; no behavior edit in Q1.
2. **Q2:** Typed one-owner GROUP-root lifecycle through normal group target resolution and final settlement; guarantee zero old-root static resurrection and correct 4s/380ms behaviour. Dedicated browser regression must fail if any frame contains an old full-size root after PARKED.
3. **Q3:** All historical PARKED cards rebase in the **first newly painted frame** after TEST SWITCH (or layout epoch change), anchor error ≤2px, no intermediate old-view flight, no lifecycle reset.
4. **Q4:** Verify *latest* H4 (minimal fix **only if reproduced**) in 4 independent authenticated Worker/D1 clients plus a separate TEST SWITCH browser. At each target step confirm narration semantics, only one current H4 banner, fixed safe position, no collision with card/history, top Seats, timers, hand and U13-B chip.
5. Capture matched **real event/card/author IDs** across clients for Barbarian Invasion **and** Raining Arrows through ≥3 actual target responses, Negation, damage and final settlement; separately test viewer switch both during initial reveal and after park, late join/refresh/resize, long names and small phones (390×700, 390×844, captured 440×766, 440×894, 480×900). Distinguish screenshots, instrumented DOM/trace, exact-SHA GitHub CI and **actual deployment**. A green build alone does not satisfy this gate.
6. **Stop conditions:** if the public linkage/author is unproven, fail closed and report—not guessed render; if Q1 shows the current release is already correct, write evidence and NO CHANGE rather than rewrite H4; if the intended fix changes CurrentAction, deck rules, hidden Negation information, existing accepted UX or server protocol, stop for review. Mark each code delivery **PENDING HUMAN REVIEW**, never “accepted” without fresh user-phone sign-off.

**Boundaries:** This specification authorizes **presentation** fixes only. Existing game's verified decisions, exact physical-card conservation, shared Negation Plan A, accepted H3 and U13-B, reveal interruption and real chronological author parking remain intact. Q1 is the sole active HANDOVER task; Q2–Q4 are future sequential tasks, **not** permission to start all four together.

**Human retest after Q1–Q4:** Observe ONE Barbarian Invasion over ALL target responses and switch TEST Player 1→2→3→4; a real root returns under REAL author exactly once, never re-enlarges/re-animates on SWITCH or target advance, all viewers see same public timeline, and narration remains in a stage-fixed safe band while U13-B accurately shows who is awaited.

### 10.16 EM-01 — safe spectator interaction (USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED, 2026-10-22)

**Accepted UX:** During Harvest/other shared choice panels, only the true server-authorized chooser may select or Confirm. Other authenticated viewers retain TEST SWITCH and harmless public Hero/Card inspection; blank overlay areas **do not trap taps**. Spectators never gain an actor's private candidates or gameplay authority. Preserve H4, real card history, server validation and privacy. Earlier diagnostics and repair steps are historical only.

### 10.17 — Battlefield Choice/HUD family (BCP-1, BCP-2, INS-5 and BH5 accepted; BCP-3 / HUD-4 delivered, pending phone review)

**Formal choices:** Product owner approves **A**, a single compact **Battlefield Choice Panel** within the play-table combat area (not a whole-screen takeover); Confirm/Use and legally required negative action belong INSIDE its footer. Product owner approves **A1** for Bumper Harvest: a compact spectator READ-ONLY view still shows the *actually public* revealed card pool and the proven taken status, while only the authorized chooser can select or Confirm. For every picker, **re-tap a selected card to deselect**. A separate **Cancel** is exceptionally rare and NOT part of default UX; do not automatically replace it with a new Clear button. Optional server-authorized Skip means formally declining a choice, and never appears alongside a redundant Cancel. The older §5.5 full-screen/modal/Cancel wording is superseded for visual presentation by this section; its authoritative selection rules still apply.

**Five screenshot review reports, NOT code acceptance:** IMG_3288(1).jpeg: Bumper Harvest large central card/card presentation obscures revealed-choice row, no clearly readable action/choice arrangement; IMG_3290.jpeg: top opponent Equipment positions shift with occupancy, small sword nearly horizontal; IMG_3292.jpeg: Sima Yi Inspector portrait too small, right details and Judgement/Equipment misallocated; IMG_3294.jpeg: Burning Bridges picker consumes too much height, controls below visible area, face-up Equipment lacks independent Info; IMG_3295.jpeg: Retaliation huge panel with Use/Skip/Cancel redundancies. The files are human UX observations; do not infer their current live build SHA or assume the source of overlapping card layers without fresh reproduction.

**EM-01 prior delivery / ownership boundary (HISTORICAL; EM-01 USER-ACCEPTED/CLOSED 2026-10-22):** EM-01 Harvest pointer pass-through was technically deployed at `32b22b829c1bfd59a4b533fa36a86b0334a3ad64`, and shared overlays at `7cc761c34ecdbbe12e7bb4af852a3975d8873206` with reported CI runs `39078356730` / `39081904968`. These are not user-approved. Preserve their TEST SWITCH, actor permission, privacy, nonmodal focus and hit-test protections. Current Agent handover still has EM-01 review open; start new UX slices at a clean boundary after reconciling latest remote source, with no worktree overwrite. Design feedback is not permission for Planner to modify HANDOVER.md or code.

#### 10.17.0 BCP-0 — common selection UX contract (ALL scoped extra-choice dialogs)

- **Physical layout:** A compact bordered dark/gold floating component positioned INSIDE the measured battle/interaction-safe region, below actual opponent seats, above the Local Dock/Hand/Guidance and outside the timer and accepted H4 public narration. No viewport-sized opaque sheet, no global touch overlay, no click intercept outside bounded controls. At 390×700 and 440×766 the footer must be visible at first view without page scrolling; large content is scrollable INSIDE the card area, preferably horizontal rows. Header one short skill/effect title + one instruction, minimal extra filler. Use consistent card aspect ratios and scales (may be smaller than ordinary hand cards, still legible through Details), close spacing and responsive safe geometry.
- **Selection:** Use server-given eligible card/opaque position keys, min/max, target, and current decision actor. Gold outline + check + modest raised position for selected. First tap adds, **repeat tap same key removes**; a different tap changes one-card choice, multi-choice selection toggles individually. Limit selection according to authoritative min/max. Selected count concise (e.g. `1 / 2`). **Confirm disabled until exactly server-permitted amount**, submits once under action revision guard. Tapping Info does NOT toggle/select/submit. No client-generated extra hidden Hand keys.
- **Buttons:** Default mandatory decision footer = **Confirm ONLY**, plus tap-to-deselect. Optional server-permitted reaction footer = **Use/Confirm + Skip ONLY**, Skip calls the exact authoritative decline action; do not show Cancel. **Do not add Clear/Reset by default.** An independently real, legitimate *abort of the whole uncommitted flow* may exceptionally use Cancel only after the Agent identifies the specific legal semantics and seeks product owner approval; do not equate Cancel with deselect or Skip. Existing internal onCancel function may remain as draft cleanup for viewer/session change, but should not be rendered as a redundant visible button. A genuinely complex drag/reorder-only Reset requires separate proof, not automatic generalization.
- **Safe viewing/privacy:** Spectators may SWITCH/Inspect real public Heroes, their own Hand Details and public Equipment where allowed, but must not gain an unauthorized selection or see hidden identities. Hand backs remain anonymous with existing `hand:n` or single random-hand server key. Information icons appear only on genuinely public identifiable choice cards and are wholly inside the card edge, accessible by touch and keyboard. Info view can close without dismissing pending choice. Respect current session/actor when switching TEST viewers: do not leak or submit stale drafts; preserve server deadlines/confirmed picks.
- **Different content, consistent frame:** Standard 1-card target picker (Steal/Burning Bridges), multi-card skill picker (Frost Sword), revealed public pool (Harvest), assignment (Legacy), rearrangement (Stargazing) may use different inner controls but the SAME title/card/footer vocabulary and bounded nonmodal geometry. Keep existing straightforward local Equipment-cost selection in Local Dock instead of forcing unnecessary duplicate popup. Actual per-effect rules and W5/W6 public effect Frame review remain distinct.

#### 10.17.1 BCP-1 — Bumper Harvest layered root vs visible pool, Info and Confirm (P0 PHONE DEFECT)

**Reproduce** with latest live Worker/D1 4-player authentic Harvest, at least two choices, one current chooser and one spectator. Capture exact DOM/paint geometry of central genuine root CardFace, public H4 narration, Harvest pool, card buttons and footer across reveal→choice→Confirm→next actor. Determine first *actual* overlap, rather than assume one z-index rule.

**Required:** Keep the true large Harvest root reveal/move valid but never paint it on TOP of active selectable public cards; stage the root and pool without overlap/duplicate fake cards. Compact the choice panel within battlefield safe space. Real revealed options fully visible/readable, horizontal swipe when necessary. Each public card has a small inset **Info** affordance that opens its genuine card description without selecting/confirming. Real selection lifts/highlights; same card re-tap deselects. A clearly visible **Confirm choice** in actor footer must stay accessible at 390×700 without any page scroll; no Cancel/Clear (choice mandatory). Taken cards become disabled/dimmed with truthful public taken-by status.
**A1 spectators:** Keep compact **read-only** public pool + taken status + safe public waiting label; no actor Confirm/Skip or private preview; allow harmless public-card Info and all SWITCH/Inspector clicks. No hidden information leakage, timers/confirmed choices unchanged.
**Acceptance:** Actual 390×700/390×844/440×766/480×900 phone-like screenshots, DOM overlap check during 2+ picks, real pointer tap on SWITCH while nonactor, Info does not change selected card, choice→re-tap→choice→one Confirm legal, no root or H4 regression.

#### 10.17.5 INS-5 — HISTORICAL parent design; refined and user-accepted under §10.17.5.1 INS-5-F1-R (NO NEW TASK)

## 11. UX3 FUTURE backlog — Negation Plan B, immediate-public-on-Confirm (NOT AN UX2 TASK)

**User preference recorded 2026-10-14 (future UX3 only):** Explore individual immediate-public Negation Confirm through a separately approved future UX3 design. This is NOT authorized autonomous UX2 work. UX2 Negation Plan A remains private all-seat submission, maximum 8s and early server settlement only after EVERY living seat explicitly responds.

**Non-trivial unresolved tradeoff:** UX2 private simultaneous intents still use canonical seat priority; all-seats explicit early completion doesn't change that. Immediately displaying an INDIVIDUAL's Confirm as a PUBLIC card, while others remain undecided, could expose a losing intent, consume a wrong card or leak secret availability. UX3 requires a separately approved authoritative arbitration rule, not an optimistic animation or simply changing the 8s maximum.

**UX3 questions to design with user before authorization:** (1) first-arrival commitment versus stable clockwise seat priority / override grace window; (2) protecting opponent Negation availability and private Hand when no card is submitted; (3) new counter windows, timeouts, fairness under network lag and reconnect; (4) atomic exactly-once actual card consumption, conflicting simultaneous confirms, Duel/Group/Judgement consistency; (5) visible chronological author-attached card history, animation interruption, old-save migration, rollbacks and authenticated multi-viewer privacy tests.

**Separate UX3 design decision, not an UX2 review gate:** UX3 needs distinct product/rules authorization because it changes priority, privacy and fairness. Continue all approved UX2 work without awaiting acceptance of prior UX2 items, but do not silently implement this unapproved UX3 rule.


### 10.24 ST-RING-DIR-1 — TOP-FIRST clockwise phone ring (USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED, 2026-10-28 08:21 UTC; 5/5 checks)

**Accepted UX:** True viewer-relative clockwise ring follows server Seat identity, the four-player reference order, TEST SWITCH and correct target/Inspector/author mapping. Phone opponent allocation is **TOP-FIRST**: **5 players = 4 top, 6 = 5 top, 7 = exactly 4 top + 1 left + 1 right; 8 = top-first, remainder on sides**. Safe spacing must protect public cards, picker, Hand, H4 and legal taps. Nine/ten-player mapping has technical tests, not independent owner-phone sign-off; 4/6/8 remains the requested visual review scope, without removing game-supported counts. **H2-G3's newer eight-player TOP5 cap and SIDE frame-width/art parity are separately scoped and not accepted by this original ring verdict.**

### 10.25 NEG-COPY-1 — Unified public Negation effects and reusable H4/PAC/Console orchestration (OWNER PHONE FAILED 2026-10-29; §10.25.3 OWNER-APPROVED DESIGN CORRECTION / NOT IMPLEMENTED)

**Authorization/identity:** `UX2-10.25-NEG-COPY-1-UNIFIED-NEGATION-EFFECT-COPY`. Approved by owner at **2026-10-28 19:03:55 UTC** (“确认方案推送”) following six genuine iPhone screenshots `IMG_3761.jpeg`, `IMG_3762.jpeg`, `IMG_3763.jpeg` (Duel), `IMG_3767.jpeg`, `IMG_3768.jpeg`, `IMG_3769.jpeg` (Harvest) and explicit final wording correction “不需要说currently 和again now”. This is a **new post-acceptance feature**, not a failure or reopening of **NEG-A1 §10.19 USER-ACCEPTED/CLOSED (6/6 on 2026-10-28 09:24:07 UTC)**. H4, BCP-1, R2, T2-F1 and other closed UX remain closed. Not an Agent dispatch, implementation, deployment or human acceptance.

**User-visible problem:** Duel currently says `Negation response open · Duel not yet resolved`, `Duel BLOCKED · Negation open` or `Another Negation is possible` without explaining cancellation/restoration. Harvest asks `Negation: Cancel Harvest for Test Player 2?` and `Counter jd's Negation?` without explaining whose **one pick** is blocked/restored. Treat screenshots as proof of **unclear public wording**, not server miscalculation or card duplication.

**Single H4 format:** Existing accepted central H4 banner, **line 1 = publicly proven event, true author and actual card/pick effect; line 2 = what ONE further Negation can do while another response is possible**. Real player names below are only examples; read server-proven identities on every frame. New visible full/compact/accessibility copy MUST NOT contain `currently`, `again`, `now`, `response open`, `not yet resolved`, `Negation open` or `Another Negation is possible`. `Another Negation can [cancel/restore/block] ...` is correct: it states actual effect. Internal protocol enum names are unaffected.

**Duel — normative English two-line copy:**

| Proven stage | Line 1 | Line 2 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Genuine root played | `jd played Duel against Test Player 2.` | `Negation can cancel the Duel.` |
| Negation #1 by Test Player 2 | `Test Player 2's Negation cancels jd's Duel.` | `Another Negation can restore the Duel.` |
| Negation #2 by jd, counters #1 | `jd counters Test Player 2's Negation. The Duel is restored.` | `Another Negation can cancel the Duel.` |
| Negation #3 by Test Player 3, counters #2 | `Test Player 3 counters jd's Negation. The Duel is cancelled.` | `Another Negation can restore the Duel.` |

**Bumper Harvest — normative transition and copy (recipient-specific, not global card cancellation):**

| Proven stage | Line 1 | Line 2 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Root card genuinely played/revealed | `jd played Bumper Harvest.` | **None during root reveal** |
| Root CardFace finishes reveal / parks at real author and public pool opens for this proven recipient | `Test Player 2's turn to pick a card.` | `Negation can prevent Test Player 2 from picking.` |
| Negation #1 by jd | `jd's Negation blocks Test Player 2's pick.` | `Another Negation can restore Test Player 2's pick.` |
| Negation #2 by Test Player 3, counters jd | `Test Player 3 counters jd's Negation. Test Player 2's pick is restored.` | `Another Negation can block Test Player 2's pick.` |
| Negation #3 by jd, counters Test Player 3 | `jd counters Test Player 3's Negation. Test Player 2's pick is blocked.` | `Another Negation can restore Test Player 2's pick.` |

**State/permission semantics:** Bumper Harvest first reveals its real CardFace and public author, then the existing genuine card moves/parks; when the **server-proven current participant** reaches the displayed pool, H4 changes to that participant's pick/Negation description without replaying the root. “Turn to pick” describes the recipient in the ordered resolution, **NOT** permission to tap while a Negation response window is open. Preserve accepted pool READ-ONLY during Negation and authorize actual `choose_harvest` only for current legitimate server actor after response settlement. An even/odd linked committed counter chain changes only that recipient's pick, never every participant's Harvest effect. Previously chosen cards, card conservation and private Hand remain unchanged.

**Provisional/final distinction without the banned filler:** While another Negation may legitimately be committed, line 2 explains its possible restoring/cancelling result, implying that the first-line effect is reversible. On **server-proven final closure**, REMOVE the “Another Negation...” line; render only truthful final copy such as `The Duel is cancelled.`, `The Duel proceeds.` (only with proven continuation), `Test Player 2 can pick a card.` (only if authorized) or `Test Player 2's Harvest pick was cancelled.` Damage, a completed pick or a victor must NEVER be asserted without separate real settlement proof. A local timer displaying zero, local Pass/selection, uncommitted/losing response or four-second reveal expiration is **not** final server evidence. A subsequent different proven recipient must replace any previous recipient's copy.

**Code-grounded diagnosis (source at approval):**
- `app/page.tsx:deriveSingleTargetNegationNarration()` hardcodes Duel `Negation response open`, `not yet resolved`, `currently`, `Another Negation is possible` in its `headline/supportingMessage/compactSupportingMessage`. The verified `singleTargetNegationProofCandidate` already has root/target, genuine counter actor and immediate linked target. Keep its proof/response/parity validation unchanged.
- `app/page.tsx:bumperHarvestRootCandidate` verifies `progress.currentParticipantId`, `currentParticipantIsProven`, `isNegationScene`, `negationProofIsValid`, genuine response `counterTarget` and public event identity. Its `harvestEffectRecipient` and `responses` are the correct public source, NOT the private user's currently selected card or the physical Seat layout.
- `app/public-card-display.tsx:isHarvestNegationPresentation()`, `bumperHarvestNegationCopy()` and `publicCardAnnouncementCopy()` use a separate one-line question path. The current `negationNarration = harvestNegationPresentation ? undefined : action.publicNegationNarration` explicitly excludes Harvest from existing proof-graded `PublicNegationNarration` two-line H4 copy. **Confirmed copy-path split**, not a server rule defect. `PublicCardAnnouncement` already supports two-line full/compact/accessibility copy and event/checkpoint ownership; reuse it.

**Bounded Coding Agent implementation at its own permitted safe task boundary:**
1. Re-read latest remote §10.25, accepted original §10.19, current `HANDOVER.md`, H4 and Bumper Harvest rules; do not preempt H2-G3 F1-R3 or other active code. Preserve old user-accepted behavior; no new unrelated feature ID.
2. Use one **small proof-graded copy helper/formatter** (shared semantic states with narrowly validated Duel and Harvest inputs) in `app/page.tsx` and the existing H4 copy path in `app/public-card-display.tsx`. Derive only from public committed root/response eventId, proven actor/immediate previous counter target, verified root effect state, current Harvest recipient and proven final state. Do not derive legality from parity alone or invent missing event/card identities. If proof insufficient, show neutral safe text without false actor, effect or finality.
3. Wire the existing Harvest **root reveal → pool open/current-recipient** transition. Keep the authentic root card, standard up-to-four-second reveal, interrupted by newer true public response, ~380ms MOVE, real author parking, no duplicate root/replay. During response, keep the pool read-only. For each genuinely committed Negation update the same H4 banner promptly; swap recipient copy on proven participant advance. On final state remove further-response sentence.
4. Apply approved normative full, compact and VoiceOver/aria wording to both Duel and Harvest, including old Harvest precedence/phase fallbacks. No internal status/jargon leaks to displayed copy, no false “pick successful” or false Duel exchange. Keep names and effect intelligible at small widths without clipped identities, tiny text, new modal, second banner, graph, or H4 position/size regression.
5. **Out of scope:** `game/`, `app/api/rooms/route.ts`, server priority or 8s/60s windows, D1, `CurrentAction` permissions, private Hand/role, CardFace identity/history/ordinals, Harvest action/choice UI, Inspector, TEST SWITCH, and **previously USER-ACCEPTED/CLOSED W3-F2-R1 announcement flicker**. Do not rework `PublicCardAnnouncement` readiness/geometry or pretend this copy change resolves flicker. If proof or accepted safe geometry cannot support required words, STOP and report specific event/DOMRect evidence for a focused owner decision.
6. Extend only the closest **existing** presentation copy/Negation/Harvest focused tests for Duel/Harvest root, #1/#2/#3 genuine responses, final outcome, spectator privacy, partial/no proof, successive real recipients, no duplicate card and no regressed local chooser. Use one authentic phone-sized regression only if needed, bounded by the current five-minute local UI budget; no 4/6/8 × device cross-product. Basic targeted tests/build/lint/diff, required exact-SHA GitHub Actions API/lint/browser smoke, Cloudflare Worker deploy and production health. Coding Agent alone records SHA, actual test/deploy/Worker results in its own `HANDOVER.md`. Technical result remains `IMPLEMENTED / DEPLOYED — PENDING OWNER REAL-iPHONE ACCEPTANCE`, never auto-accepted.

**Owner phone acceptance:** Duel root/#1/#2 (optional #3), Harvest root/park/pool/current-recipient/#1/#2 (optional #3), actual next-recipient switch and terminal settlement. Public copy must consistently explain **actor, root/recipient effect, and the meaning of another Negation**, with no banned filler in large/compact/accessible text; H4 has no new overlay, card duplication or misleading FINAL. TEST SWITCH/other viewers agree on proven public copy, no leakage of private intent/Hand, no premature Harvest pick, and all earlier NEG-A1 6/6, BCP-1 and H4 phone passes are preserved. **Only a later explicit owner verdict closes NEG-COPY-1; no next review/task is started by this documentation commit.**

This is SAME ORIGINAL NEG-COPY-1 repair, not a new requirement family and not a basis to unaccept old projects. No new Agent action starts merely because this document was edited. The original §10.25 “do not rework H4 readiness/geometry” restriction applies to its initial WORDING-only implementation; it is **narrowly superseded by this owner-evidenced same-original F1-R2/F1-R3 geometry regression repair**, not a general license to redesign H4.

**Evidence ledger — precise boundary between CONFIRMED and inference:**

Card author/target geometry changes legitimately between viewers, but public **semantic result** must not change by viewpoint. Screenshots alone do NOT show server rule incorrectness. |

**Normative product contract, approved extension to §10.25:**

1. The **server-proven presentation stage is the current interaction owner**. The root event identity is stable through response, but root/Negation H4 copy must be owned by the current proven `NEGATION` response stage and final settlement transition. On proven `DUEL_EXCHANGE`, the stage immediately takes ownership of the public interaction; do **not** restore `played Duel on ...` or keep `The Duel takes effect.` as a persistent H4 banner. The existing truthful `PublicActivityChip` (`Waiting for X · Duel Exchange`) and real parked Duel/Negation/Attack history express the ongoing exchange; the one final Negation result may be read briefly only during an actual verified *handoff state*, never pinned through exchange. No arbitrary seconds-long timer is allowed to hide a stale lifecycle bug.
2. **Semantic viewpoint invariance:** For the SAME `room + interactionId + rootFrameId + authoritative presentation checkpoint/revision`, the public `actor/root/counter/effect/phase` meaning must be identical for every viewer. Physical parked-card coordinates, labels such as “Your response”, active controls and permitted Hand identities can differ by viewer. Do not derive public copy from TEST SWITCH viewer, rotating visual seats, private CurrentAction or a stale saved `activePublicNegationRead`.
3. **Monotonic phase precedence for the same interaction:** `ROOT_REVEAL → NEGATION_WINDOW → [PROVEN_SETTLEMENT_HANDOFF] → DUEL_EXCHANGE → DUEL_COMPLETE` (Duel path), with separate `ROOT_REVEAL → recipient-specific NEGATION_WINDOW → [PROVEN_SETTLEMENT_HANDOFF] → HARVEST_CHOICE / NEXT_RECIPIENT → HARVEST_COMPLETE` (Harvest path). These are DISPLAY ownership stages, **not new server protocol enums**; derive transitions from existing proven snapshot/action/event IDs. A later stage wins; polling, TEST SWITCH, local 0s, repeat layouts or an older parked history MUST NOT regress the current stage. A new interaction or new server-proven recipient resets only its own stage/identity. If proof temporarily disappears during transition, preserve only the last verified identity/last safe **presentation status** for the same live interaction; do not fabricate an older stage, new participant or FINAL.
4. A public H4 banner is stage-owned, not merely the latest visible CardFace. When `DUEL_EXCHANGE` begins, the prior public Negation narration RETIRES from active H4 without deleting the historical actual card faces. If the current stage requires no H4, render none rather than fallback to a root sentence. No duplicate H4 presenters, no simultaneous stale semantic claims. The original §10.25 player-visible vocabulary ban (`currently`, `again`, `now` and uninformative internal status terms) and complete actor/effect/next-effect wording remain in effect DURING genuine open Negation windows.
5. A stable single H4 announcement for a semantic event remains **visible and at one valid geometry** across routine identical polls and measurement cycles. Reposition only when a real layout epoch, public event identity, genuine obstacle occupancy, or measured safe-area constraint changes; record reason. Valid safety overrides (new card/history, TEST SWITCH seat topology, viewport change) can move it but may not silently replace semantic copy, overlap controls, or duplicate cards. Small-font or truncation shortcuts are not acceptable.
6. The lower **`PublicActivityChip`** is a different component from H4 and may legitimately move to avoid collision. Prefer centered lower-band placement when it is both safe and stable. If center is unsafe, choose the nearest legal alternate and KEEP that valid choice until the occupancy/layout epoch materially changes; do not jump on every mutation. If no legal location fits, fail closed with diagnostic evidence rather than cover Hand, Dock controls, timer or genuine parked CardFace. Preserve existing H4 public-band and activity-chip visuals (color, type, touch blocking) and the tested player-count/Seat topology safety; only correct evidenced placement and lifecycle behavior.

**Coding Agent execution contract: three sequential, independently testable bounded steps INSIDE one original NEG-COPY-1 identity.** The Agent is LESS CAPABLE than Planner: follow these exact ownership/validation requirements, do not silently redesign gameplay. Wait until existing compatible `HANDOVER.md` task (currently H2-G3 F1-R4 awaiting owner review) has a safe boundary; this design approval does NOT authorize replacing HANDOVER or modifying that other feature. Branch `ux-v2`, read latest `AGENTS.md`, `docs/PLANNER_REVIEWER_GUIDE.md`, §10.25, original NEG-A1, server proof projection and actual handover before work. No `main`, force push, reset, clean or unrelated edits.

**STEP F1-R1 (P1, MUST FINISH FIRST): correct public stage authority and Duel Exchange handoff.**

At **19:31:01.669 UTC**, \`presentation-dom.cardInstances\` contains **FIVE instances for THREE unique physical event IDs**: root Duel and two Negations, with BOTH Negation events simultaneously duplicated between authentic \`duel-journey\` author-parked history and LEGACY \`table-resolution-settled\` centre thumbnails. **Current source finding / causal candidate:** \`app/page.tsx\` computes \`sequenceOwnerEventIds\` and \`sequenceOwnerCardIds\` from \`publicCardDisplayOwnsPresentation\`, but otherwise only excludes a narrower completed Attack/Dodge set; \`displayedSequenceEvents\` may feed \`TableResolutionSequence\` independently of the authentic \`PublicCardDisplay\` Duel/Negation journey. \`TableResolutionSequence\` explicitly renders legacy \`table-resolution-settled\` thumbnails. Transient snapshot/ownership mismatch at \`NEGATION → DUEL_EXCHANGE → DAMAGE/trigger\` may allow the same public physical ID to return through the legacy route while it still has true journey ownership. **This is a confirmed code mechanism plus older-trace duplicate, not proven as the later post-damage root cause.** Do not blindly disable the legacy sequence for Judgement/equipment/independent events or rely on CSS to hide an unproven card.

**F1-R4 (P1; only after F1-R2/R3 safe completion): enforce one physical CardFace owner and correct Duel→damage/Treachery retirement.** Build ONE focused real 4-client Worker/D1 fixture: printed Duel root → genuinely committed Negation → committed counter → root restored → first/second real Duel Attack → failed Attack → 1 actual Duel damage → genuine Cao Cao Treachery/Skip child continuation. After proven Duel settlement and the legitimate ~2s readable result/retirement, the next Treachery decision remains available for its full **existing** authorized timer and the old root+Attack must NOT persist as a grouped central pair for 14s. If remaining cards are genuinely needed during child continuation, they must stay *briefly, safely and distinctly under true authors*, not centrally as an orphaned gallery.

For each NEW genuine Duel Attack, the existing ONE H4 event banner may show e.g. \`Test Player 3 answered the Duel with Attack.\` and, ONLY if publicly proven on that same active checkpoint, \`Test Player 2 must answer with Attack.\` Never interpret an Attack response as a new independent Attack target or assign author/next responder from alternating guesses, DOM order, private \`CurrentAction\`, stale event or visual seat position. If no new event is public, chip alone may express waiting and H4 may be absent; no root/Negation replay. ### 10.25.3 NEG-COPY-1 F1-R6 — OWNER-APPROVED reusable H4 / PAC / Console presentation contract, including damage-triggered Hero skills (2026-10-29 18:42 UTC; DESIGN APPROVED / R6A-O1 AND R6B TECHNICALLY DEPLOYED; R6C NEXT; OWNER REVIEW PENDING)

**Owner verdict and precedence:** Real-iPhone owner test on 2026-10-29 at running build \`79ca1bab9efedf98f5467b03e9b965429663fb9f\` FAILED the original §10.25 visual/copy/lifecycle review; the previous F1-R1–F1-R5 deployments are technical history, **NOT USER ACCEPTANCE**. Owner approved this revised presentation solution on 2026-10-29 18:42 UTC, with a **binding requirement**: no card-specific, Hero-specific or skill-specific control-flow forks in the shared presentation implementation. This subsection **supersedes conflicting copy-placement instructions** above (especially the old two-line H4 and the old combined Duel Attack message), but preserves earlier user-accepted card mechanics, H4 styling, local permissions, Negation privacy and physical parking. Do not create a second NEG-COPY-1 ticket, change rules or mark owner acceptance.

#### HARD architectural acceptance gate — generic, reusable and capability-driven, never name-special-cased

**MUST:** All H4 / PAC / Console stage/copy orchestration, public-event retention, post-damage skill handoff, card-history ownership and geometry rules operate on a **generic public event + public next-decision + viewer-local authorized choice** contract. They MUST NOT depend on a card's name/id/kind (e.g. \`Duel\`, \`BarbarianInvasion\`), Hero id/name (e.g. \`cao-cao\`, \`simayi\`) or specific skill id (e.g. \`cao_cao_jianxiong\`, \`sima_yi_fankui\`) in central/shared presentation components or orchestration. Reject fixes consisting of \`if (card.kind === "Duel")\`, \`if (hero.id === "cao-cao")\`, \`switch(skillId)\`, per-card/per-Hero hardcoded copy, or one-off exceptions in \`app/page.tsx\`, \`app/public-card-display.tsx\`, \`app/public-activity-chip.tsx\`, \`game/public-activity.ts\` or another shared presentation coordinator. Do not merely rename these branches or hide them in a helper.

**Important domain boundary:** Actual card/Hero mechanics are necessarily individual: isolated **capability/provider** modules MAY implement their own unique rules, legality and outcomes; they register facts/semantic descriptors into common interfaces. The **shared presentation pipeline must not inspect those provider identities** to determine copy, H4/PAC/Console ownership, stage transitions, expiry or layout. A provider can supply a public action label/effect descriptor and server-verified outcome, not a custom React branch, free-form private-data leak or a raw transport-state string. The existing \`game/capabilities/triggers.ts\` / \`TriggeredEffect\` / \`TriggerOption\` / \`TriggerExecutionOutcome\` illustrate a suitable capability boundary; extend existing public projection carefully rather than duplicating the game engine or imposing premature protocol enums.

**Shared semantic data contract (conceptual, not a mandate to use these exact TypeScript names):**
- **Public event fact:** immutable interaction/root/event identity and checkpoint/revision; publicly proven actor, affected players, category \`CARD_PLAYED | EFFECT_CANCELLED | EFFECT_RESTORED | DAMAGE_APPLIED | SKILL_USED | CARD_OBTAINED | CARD_TRANSFERRED | ...\`, permitted public card/skill label, count/amount, source/cause, visibility/proof quality, and a semantic event ordering key. New action/effect categories are generic **meaning types**, not named card/Hero branches.
- **Public next action:** server-proven current stage label and its publicly visible next actor(s), required action/outcome options, whether another response/counter is still permitted, and public/private visibility. This is the ONLY source for shared PAC text and generic phase continuation; a previous root must not reacquire ownership from a stale poll or a visual CardFace.
- **Viewer-local choice:** server-authorized \`CurrentAction\`/legal options, selection constraints, Skip/Pass/Take Damage, confirmed own locked selection and timer; it drives only the current viewer's Console and any expressly private PAC acknowledgment. Must not promote private choices, Hand identities or eligibility to the shared public projection.
- **Presentation lifecycle:** \`event -> effect -> waiting/decision -> next genuine event\` and \`damage applied -> optional skill decision -> public skill effect OR Skip -> next verified action\` are generic, identity/revision-guarded transitions. H4, PAC and physical CardFace each have independent ownership/retirement. Retain the last relevant **proven** H4 event during a child skill decision; replace it only with a newer proven public event. Don't replay retired root/card history or manufacture public effects during temporary proof gaps.

**Shared copy placement:**
1. **H4 = WHAT HAPPENED.** Report only the last actual public event, its actor and concrete effect. One public event statement; no unrelated next-player instruction. A newly and publicly committed Attack example: \`Jdhs played Attack in the Duel.\`. No publicly committed next Attack means no invented H4. Preserve \`The Duel is restored.\` while waiting for the first Duel Attack **if restoration actually occurred**; if no Negation occurred, preserve the original genuine \`Jdhs played Duel against Test Player 4.\` instead. While a damage-triggered Hero decision is open, H4 holds the proven damage event rather than returning to Duel/Attack or disappearing.
2. **PAC = WHAT HAPPENS NEXT / PUBLIC STAGE.** Centered in the lower battlefield, with \`Duel Exchange\` kept as a stage label throughout the proven exchange and the server-proven next action e.g. \`Test Player 4 must play Attack or take damage.\`. During open Negation it describes the effect of one further Negation (\`Negation can cancel the Duel.\` / \`Another Negation can restore the Duel.\`) and the neutral shared waiting phase; after final Duel outcome it advances to the true next stage. After a genuine Duel ending it can display \`Duel ended.\`; during a publicly proven Hero trigger decision it changes to \`Waiting for <player> to use <skill> or Skip.\`, with only \`Waiting for <player>.\` if skill identity/eligibility is not publicly proven. Do not retain \`Duel Exchange\` after the exchange actually ends.
3. **Console = WHAT CAN I DO.** Only the current player with a verified legal decision sees extra action-specific guidance/controls; in a two-player Duel Exchange all **nonparticipant** Consoles do NOT display extra Duel instructions or duplicate PAC. Authorized actor sees \`Play Attack or take damage.\`; eligible Negation actors see \`Play Negation [to cancel/restore] or Pass.\`; eligible damage-trigger owner sees \`Use <skill> or Skip.\` plus generic provider-supplied choice/target requirements. Disabled/unavailable choices must never appear enabled.
4. **Private Negation acknowledgement:** After the local viewer has verifiably locked a Negation play, the **local-only PAC** may show \`You played Negation. Waiting for the other players.\` (and analogous locally proven Pass copy), without publishing that decision to another viewer. Do not infer Play vs Pass from the generic \`submitted\` flag alone; use an actual reliable viewer-private choice record or neutral \`Waiting for the other players.\`. No visible \`submitted\`, \`request\`, \`processing\` or other transport implementation jargon.
5. **Proven counter chain:** for root Duel / public Negation #1 / #2 / #3 the H4 carries the actual public played/cancelled/restored fact; PAC carries the *next* possible cancellation/restoration only while further countering is truly open. On final settlement PAC must not imply another response. Every viewer sees identical shared public meaning for identical authority, regardless of TEST SWITCH, card parking or private state. Preserve existing recipient-specific Bumper Harvest effects and eligibility with the same **generic** event/effect/next-action machinery; don't instantiate a separate Harvest-only H4/PAC path.
6. **Physical layout:** PAC is horizontally centered in the lower battlefield within **±4 CSS px** when no protected full-sized REVEAL/active controls/timer/Inspector/H4 conflicts exist. It MAY cover genuine PARKED **thumbnail cards** and must paint above them; thumbnail or projected parking occupancy alone MUST NOT push PAC right/left or hide it. It must not cover interactive controls, full-size active CardFace, decision timers or Inspector. H4 must avoid all *actually visible* cards, including current PARKED Attack, and remain stable across benign polls and TEST SWITCH. Do not apply global z-index, timeout, or CSS resets.

#### Reusable end-to-end examples (fixtures / wording examples, NOT dispatch conditions)

| Server-proven transition | H4 (public fact; retained as specified) | PAC (next public action) | Console (eligible viewer only) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| New root played; counter window open | \`Jdhs played Duel against Test Player 4.\` | \`Negation can cancel the Duel.\` + neutral response waiting | Legal Negation/Pass, if eligible |
| Viewer has privately and verifiably chosen Negation; still waiting | Same root public event | **Only that viewer:** \`You played Negation. Waiting for the other players.\`; others unchanged | Choice locked; no duplicate submission |
| First committed public counter | \`Test Player 4's Negation cancels Jdhs's Duel.\` | \`Another Negation can restore the Duel.\` while open | Legal counter/Pass |
| Second committed public counter | \`Jdhs counters Test Player 4's Negation. The Duel is restored.\` | \`Another Negation can cancel the Duel.\` while open | Legal counter/Pass |
| Final public cancellation | \`The Duel is cancelled.\` | Next true game phase, not Duel Exchange | True next legal actor only |
| Proven restoration -> first response pending | **Retain** \`The Duel is restored.\` until a new real public event | \`Duel Exchange\` + \`Test Player 4 must play Attack or take damage.\` | Only Test Player 4: Attack/Take Damage |
| No Negation -> first response pending | **Retain** original played-Duel H4; never claim restoration | Same generic exchange stage + proven actor | Only proven exchange responder |
| Real exchange Attack by Jdhs | \`Jdhs played Attack in the Duel.\` | \`Duel Exchange\` + \`Test Player 4 must play Attack or take damage.\` | Only Test Player 4: Attack/Take Damage |
| Exchange settles with actual damage | \`Test Player 4 took 1 damage from Jdhs in the Duel.\` (only with full source/amount proof) | \`Duel ended.\` | No invented next decision |
| Publicly known damage-triggered Hero decision opens | **Retain prior damage H4** | \`Waiting for Test Player 4 to use Treachery or Skip.\` (only when publicly proven) | Only skill owner: Use/Skip and any real selection |
| Confirmed skill actually obtains visible card(s) | \`Test Player 4 used Treachery and obtained the Duel card.\` **only if that exact card/effect is public** | Next proven actor/stage | Retire completed choice |
| Skill skipped | **Retain last damage H4** (or latest newer proven event) | Next proven actor/stage | Retire completed choice |
| A DIFFERENT victim with a DIFFERENT registered damage-triggered skill | Preserve actual victim/source/amount damage H4; then actual proven skill effect | Generic waiting stage for the true deciding player/skill, then genuine next action | Owner-specific provider choice or Skip; other players' Consoles unchanged |

**Multiple post-damage skills:** Treat chained/repeated trigger opportunities as an ordered series of independent genuine decisions and resulting public events. At each wait, preserve the latest relevant proven H4, update PAC to the newly proven action owner (not the prior Duel actor), and authorize only the actual local decision. A skill with another-player follow-up or Judgement uses the same generic decision stage. Concealed Hand card identities, uncommitted provider eligibility and secret intent never become public. This works equally for Retaliation (take a permitted card from the damage source, possibly hidden), Legacy (can repeat per point of damage), Stauchness (subsequent Judgement/source choice), and any future damage-trigger provider **without adding those identities to shared presentation conditions**.

**Source-grounded gaps to resolve, not shortcuts:** Current \`game/public-activity.ts:responseLabel()\` explicitly branches on \`DUEL_EXCHANGE\` and named effect strings, \`game/duel-public-stage-owner.ts:duelExchangeAttackNarration()\` builds Duel-only combined H4 copy, and \`app/page.tsx:consoleInstruction\` / \`RESPONSE SUBMITTED\` include implementation language. These are **existing patterns that must not be extended** for this work; extract reusable evidence/semantic projection and general formatting instead. Existing damage capability providers (\`game/capabilities/triggers.ts\`, \`game/capabilities/heroes/*\`) own actual rules and outcomes; the stage projection must expose sufficient **public evidence** for skill-use/effect/obtained-card announcements. If required public proof or privacy classification is missing, use the explicitly APPROVED minimal server projection extension specified in the 2026-10-29 22:47 ruling below; STOP only when exact proof is still unavailable even after that bounded extension. Never use a card-name/Hero-name branch in React to guess it.

#### 2026-10-29 22:47 UTC — Planner technical ruling for R6A: existing authority + MINIMAL server-owned projection extension (APPROVED, NOT IMPLEMENTED)

**Scope of this ruling:** Owner-approved §10.25.3 UX is already fixed; the Coding Agent asked one narrow question: which authoritative server sources can supply events, sequencing, outcomes, next-action semantics, and a viewer's own private Negation/Pass selection, including nested post-damage triggers. **DECISION: reuse the existing persisted event and causal/action authorities, and AUTHORIZE a minimal typed, read-only server presentation projection extension where the current public contract is insufficient.** This is an authorization for R6A's technical contract, not a mandate to rebuild the game engine, add a second event store, change legal actions or timers, introduce a per-card/Hero UX branch, or mark R6 delivered. The Agent's uncommitted local proposal at \`/Users/jingedai/.codex/worktrees/h2-g3-f1-r5/WTK/docs/UX2-refine.md:3848\` was **not available to the Reviewer**, so this ruling does not assert its exact schema was inspected or approved wholesale. The Agent must reconcile that local diff with this canonical remote chapter without overwriting other work.

**(A) Source of public committed events and safe event order**
- **Authority:** \`rooms.log_json\` persisted by \`app/api/rooms/route.ts\` through common \`addLog[WithId]\`, \`addCardEvent[WithId]\`, committed outcome paths, and filtered \`gameTimeline(rawLog, viewerId)\`; \`PresentationV2Event\` in \`game/presentation-v2.ts\` already carries IDs, \`resolutionId\`, public/card markers and some typed result proofs. \`projectPresentationV2()\` and \`composePresentationSnapshot()\` validate the current publicly proven interaction/frame/checkpoint/revision. The server event-commit path owns *when* a fact becomes real; the snapshot/admission layer owns whether that fact is publicly safe to announce.
- **Missing generic fact projection:** \`game/presentation-snapshot.ts\` currently has \`transitionEvents: readonly []\` and literally sets \`transitionEvents: []\` in \`composePresentationSnapshot()\`; \`game/presentation-v2.ts\` already has a different, narrowly scoped list of timeline-linked transition IDs, but neither constitutes an approved generic semantic outcome/history contract. Existing specialized settlement projections and English \`message\` strings are not sufficient for generic H4 retention and skill outcome copy.
- **Approved addition:** an additive, typed, public \`eventFacts\` / \`transitionEvents\`-equivalent on the existing snapshot, each from a **server-committed** public action/outcome with stable \`eventId\`, appropriate \`resolutionId\`/\`rootEventId\`, causal \`interactionId\`, \`frameId\`/\`parentFrameId\`, checkpoint/revision when available, generic semantic kind, actor/affected parties, proven amount, and only publicly revealable effect/card descriptors. Generic kinds include committed play, counter/cancel/restore, damage applied, skill activated, obtained/transferred card, recovery, judgement and final settlement. Provider-specific mechanics stay in providers; no shared renderer switch on \`Duel\`, \`cao-cao\`, \`Treachery\` etc. A semantic event whose actor/result/card visibility is not proven **must omit or redact** that field. Do not parse historical English strings to infer outcome or derive a revealed card from the visible DOM.
- **Ordering:** the existing durable append order plus immutable public event IDs gives authoritative local sequence within the retained log. Because \`log_json\` is bounded (\`slice(-199)\`), an array index or client timestamp is **not** a durable event identity/ordinal across truncation/reconnect. If a stable cross-window order key is truly needed, assign it at a common server event-commit boundary using a persisted monotonic sequence/committed causal marker, not a viewer clock or renderer order; do **not** introduce a new event store by default. Reject stale/mismatched identity or lower revision; never resurface a retired physical card by replaying an old event projection.

**(B) Source of public next decision / PAC**
- **Authority:** the persisted server \`Pending\` / \`TriggerPending\` (including actor, stage, deadline and continuation), canonical \`CausalEnvelope\` / \`CausalContext\`, existing \`PresentationV2.interactionScene\`, and validated \`PresentationSnapshot.identity / interaction / stable / decision\`. \`game/public-activity.ts:derivePublicActivityStatus()\` currently reads these *partly*, but \`responseLabel()\` switches on named stages/effects; it is not sufficient as the reusable next-action contract.
- **Approved addition:** server-owned, generic \`publicNextDecision\` projected/validated alongside the existing snapshot: current public stage label; publicly admissible actor(s), publicly admissible action/choice label(s) and expected next effect; identity/checkpoint/revision; visibility/redaction policy. Do not build it from a spectator's \`CurrentAction\`, client private hand or guesses about alternating players. **Public** skill names and actor IDs are emitted only when their availability and identity are proven public; use \`triggerAvailability()\` and \`privateOpportunityWindowEffectIds()\` semantics. A private trigger must continue to use a truthful redacted PAC (possibly neutral \`Resolving…\` where explicitly required by already accepted privacy behavior), not leak skill, player or eligibility. The Console uses viewer-local \`CurrentAction\` independently. PAC's local-private acknowledgement is a separate optional viewer override, not a mutation of the public decision object.
- **Lifecycle:** generic \`publicNextDecision\` changes on a server-accepted stage/action transition, not on DOM geometry. It represents the actual next chooser and options even when the current event is a child reaction. The \`Duel Exchange\` heading is a **server/public semantic label**, not a frontend \`if (card === Duel)\`; after exchange settlement, the next decision replaces it. The generic PAC can display \`Duel ended.\` as an admitted settlement/status, then a publicly eligible next skill chooser.

**(C) Negation/Pass private selection — DB already provides reliable proof**
- **CONFIRMED:** \`app/api/rooms/route.ts:NegationWindowSubmission\` and \`parseNegationWindowSubmission()\` distinguish persisted \`{kind:"pass"}\` from \`{kind:"intent", providerId, cardIds}\`. The action path validates and writes to D1 \`negation_window_submissions\` under \`(room_id, window_id, player_id)\`; \`roomState()\` already queries **only the current authenticated \`me.id\` row** but mainly exposes \`pendingNegation.submitted: boolean\`, losing the category needed for honest PAC copy.
- **Approved addition:** a viewer-only, current-window identity-bound \`localNegationChoice\` (or equivalent) containing only \`windowId\`, \`locked: boolean\`, and a coarse \`PASS | NEGATION_INTENT\` category. Never send \`providerId\`, \`cardIds\`, other players' submissions, seat-order winner/loser or eligibility in a shared field; validate TEST SWITCH \`requestedViewerId\` belongs to the authenticated controlled session. Preserve choice after refresh while the same window is still open, discard it when the window changes/closes. An intent is **not a committed public Negation** and may fail final arbitration. Consequently a truthful personal acknowledgment is \`You chose to play Negation. Waiting for the other players.\` (or another owner-equivalent local-only wording), and \`You passed. Waiting for the other players.\` only after verified local Pass. When the kind cannot be proven, use neutral \`Waiting for the other players.\`. Existing response lock and 8-second deadline are unchanged.

**(D) Capability/provider ownership and survival across nested skill decisions**
- **Authority for game semantics:** \`game/capabilities/triggers.ts\` (\`TriggeredEffect.getOption\`, \`resolveTriggeredEffect\`, \`TriggerOption\`, \`TriggerExecutionOutcome\`, \`triggerAvailability\`) plus the existing server commit branches in \`app/api/rooms/route.ts\`. \`TriggerExecutionOutcome.kind\` is a **domain operation**, not proof that the final public result has already committed. The server must emit a generic typed public fact only **after** it validates and commits the actual effect, including exact card-zone movement and public identity eligibility. Existing \`effectNotice\` and natural-language logs (\`... resolves an optional reaction ...\`, \`... with Treachery\` or \`... with Retaliation\`) do not by themselves prove the full actual public result.
- **Causal continuity:** reuse persisted \`CausalContext.interactionId/frameId/parentFrameId\`, \`DamageSufferedTriggerContinuation\` (damage source/target/amount/rootEventId, \`resolvedEffectIds\`, \`resolvedDamagePointEffectIds\`), and \`resumeDamageSuffered / resumeGroup / resumeTurnEnd\`. \`continueDamageSufferedEvent()\`, \`finishDamageSufferedEvent()\` and related judgement/distribution continuations already iterate/resume capabilities and sometimes repeat once per damage point. The minimal projection must link a committed damage event to its subsequent optional skill-window and effect outcome and retain the **last verified H4 damage fact** while a child choice is active. On a genuine skill effect, replace H4 with the actual public outcome; on Skip, do **not** invent a skill event, keep damage or newer legitimate public event, then update PAC from the true next decision. For a chain of eligible providers, use event ancestry and the new verified chooser, never hero identity or a fixed assumption about one optional skill.
- **Privacy/negative cases:** obtaining a card from hidden Hand can publicly state a one-card transfer with redacted face; gaining a proven face-up card may name it only if that face is genuinely public. A provider's optional skill label must not become public simply because the private actor can see \`CurrentAction.triggerOptions\`. Keep existing accepted private opportunity windows and prohibition against private intent exposure.

**Independent source review — confidence classification, assessed at remote HEAD \`e118c5e30a83a9e0c5f7e83689188e994ebdaa3b\`:**
| Review item | Classification | Evidence / implication |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Committed log and bounded per-viewer timeline | **CONFIRMED SOURCE FACT** | \`app/api/rooms/route.ts:gameTimeline()\`, \`addLog[WithId]()\`, \`roomState()\` and \`game/presentation-v2.ts:projectPresentationV2()\`; event order and public results must be server-validated |
| Coherent current scene but no admitted generic historical outcome stream | **CONFIRMED SOURCE GAP** | \`game/presentation-snapshot.ts:composePresentationSnapshot()\` yields \`transitionEvents: []\`; typed specialist proofs exist but no universal H4 result/retention contract |
| Personal Negation category persisted but absent from local presentation | **CONFIRMED SOURCE GAP** | D1 \`negation_window_submissions\` stores \`pass/intent\`; \`roomState()\` projects only \`submitted\`; a minimal viewer-only category field resolves ambiguity |
| Public decision formatter depends on named effect/stage | **CONFIRMED SOURCE COUPLING** | \`game/public-activity.ts:responseLabel()\`; migrate touched routing to generic public semantic decision rather than add more identity conditionals |
| Post-damage options and nested resumption already exist | **CONFIRMED SOURCE FACT** | \`continueDamageSufferedEvent()\`, \`finishDamageSufferedEvent()\`, \`game/pending.ts:DamageSufferedTriggerContinuation\`; retain ancestry rather than invent a new skill state machine |
| Existing skill outcome texts are enough for every future H4 | **NOT ESTABLISHED / DO NOT ASSUME** | Individual server branches and \`effectNotice\` mix labels, private draws and physical-zone outcomes; require semantic commit proof before announcing exact cards |
| Inspector itself triggers central miniature grouping | **NOT PROVEN** | Owner screenshot reports intermittence; the 2026-10-29 trace confirms stale legacy gallery at the later \`DUEL_EXCHANGE -> DAMAGE\` handoff, but an Inspector click alone was not causally established |

**R6A stop/go, acceptance and exact request to Agent:** This is the **bounded minimal projection change already authorized**. Implement at the existing server emission/typed projection/admission boundary, retain backwards-compatible fields and server-enforced legal decisions; do not add another database selection store, alter action rules, or branch by named card/Hero in shared renderers. Cover (1) committed public outcome vs uncommitted intent, (2) public sequencing and stale checkpoint protection, (3) two authenticated viewers plus TEST SWITCH privacy, (4) current-window Pass versus Negation-intent category, (5) damage H4 retention across optional/nested/repeated trigger decision and Skip, (6) actual skill result with known public card versus hidden card, and (7) at least one synthetic previously unknown provider with no shared-display source change. Use focused tests and ≤5 minutes local UI/browser time per bounded stage, then normal exact-SHA CI/deployment verification. **If these facts cannot be produced even with the approved minimal extension, stop with the exact missing server proof field and independent review request; never improvise in the client.** R6B–R6D remain the **same four sequential scopes in §10.25.3**, not four new top-level tickets. \`NEG-COPY-1\` remains **OWNER NOT ACCEPTED / CORRECTION REQUIRED** until a later real-iPhone verdict.

**Coordination / review boundary:** This edit changes **only the canonical design chapter**. The Coding Agent owns source edits, actual tests, its own \`HANDOVER.md\`, and any uncommitted local proposal/worktree; Planner does not overwrite or dispatch it from this documentation update. The Agent should read this ruling at its next safe boundary and reconcile its current partial R6A implementation. No implementation, test pass, deployment or user acceptance is claimed here.
